# §11 — Phases shipped

Moved out of `CLAUDE.md` on 2026-09-23. This is a **log**: 168 chronological rows of
work that shipped, plus the cross-cutting architectural finding §11 recorded about
`sanitizeInput`. Historical by construction — nothing here is a standing rule, and a
session not researching what shipped when does not need it loaded.

It was 28% of a file injected into every session and every subagent's system prompt.
Section numbers are unchanged; see `../README.md`.

---

| v3.8.ahm | (this commit) | **Caravan Journey rebuild (Path A, fresh from intent)** — replaces the v3.8.ahi/ahj/ahk/ahl iteration arc (highway → parchment → camel → hotfixes) with a clean spec-driven component. Dual gold-dark dashed lane with stroke-dashoffset flow animation; 4 brass marker cartouches (M0 Apply / M1—M3 Silver / M4 Gold / M5 Platinum); navy Cascadia tractor-trailer SVG with cream "SRL" decal travels lane via `left` transition; "QP LOCKED" plaque (M6 sub-state, never a 5th tier) stamps onto Platinum post during the 4s hold. Single source of truth: `cjPhase` index (0-3) drives tab + marker + panel + truck-position + plaque-stamp via `cjSetPhase()`. Bidirectional: tab clicks drive truck to that waypoint. Auto-loops Apply→Platinum in 24s (8s/segment × 3), holds 4s at Platinum, resets. prefers-reduced-motion freezes at Platinum with plaque visible. Mobile rotates lane vertical, truck travels via `top` transition, tab row becomes horizontal scroll strip. Font-load halt: console.error if Playfair Display fails so labels can't garble. All §2.1 tokens only (navy #0A2540 / gold #C5A572 / gold-dark #BA7517 / cream #FBF7F0 / cream-2 #F5EEE0). New `.cj-*` namespace replaces old `.journey-*` + `.caravansary-*` + `.compass-traveler` classes entirely. Letter coordination note: ahj/ahk/ahl were used twice across this thread (Caravan Journey iterations) and the parallel Authority-age compliance epic (Item 182). Authority-age epic's reserved ahj/ahk/ahl/ahm/ahn range pushes its sprint 3+ to next-free letters after this docs catch-up. |
| v3.8.ahp | (this commit) | **Caravan Journey direction fixes** — two-issue surgical hotfix on the v3.8.ahm component, scoped per Wasi directive (halt-and-report if exceeded 4-file/100-line threshold; landed at ~35 LOC across 4 files). (1) SRL truck silhouette previously rendered cab-on-left (facing west) while traveling east — flipped via internal `<g transform="scale(-1, 1) translate(-140, 0)">` so it now faces direction of travel. SRL wordmark `<text>` rendered OUTSIDE the flip group as its own non-mirrored layer per directive ("render it in its own layer that does not mirror when the silhouette flips") so the wordmark stays readable left-to-right. Center coord adjusted x=73→x=67 to land on the flipped trailer center. (2) Competitor truck moved from upper lane (top:30px) to LOWER lane (top:160px) and `CJ_COMPETITOR_POS` reversed from `['0%','20%','38%','58%']` to `['90%','70%','45%','20%']` so it now travels WESTBOUND opposite the eastbound SRL. Road now reads as genuine two-way traffic. (3) Reset-snap mechanism added — new `cjSnapReset()` adds `.is-resetting` class (CSS rule `transition: none !important`) before the Platinum→Apply teleport, removes on double-rAF. Without this, both trucks would visibly drive in reverse for 1.8s during the rewind. Satisfies "never point backward at any point in the loop". §5 preserved (competitor remains unnamed/un-marked). |

Cash comes back faster."; subtitle rewritten to lead with Quick Pay value prop. (2) Lane background — cream→navy `linear-gradient(180deg, #0A2540 0%, #061629 100%)` with brass border. (4) Lane labels — "Trade Route" (single italic) replaced by `OUTBOUND FREIGHT` (top-left) + `QUICK PAY RETURNING` (bottom-right) framing the two-way narrative. (5) Competitor truck (anonymous gray Cascadia) → gold "$" coin with `linear-gradient(#E8D4A8→#C5A572→#BA7517)` and gentle glow-pulse animation. Coin = Quick Pay returning to carrier. | v3.8.ahz | (this commit) | **CeoOverview Run-FMCSA-Scan button + last-run summary card (Item 188 frontend half)** — closes Item 188 atomic-pair from v3.8.ahy. **Changes** (single file, ~110 LOC net): [`frontend/src/components/dashboard/CeoOverview.tsx`](frontend/src/components/dashboard/CeoOverview.tsx). New section 2.5 between Team Performance (Section 2) and Needs Attention (Section 3), sitting visually next to the "Compliance Alerts Active" tile in the Carrier Ops column above per directive ("near the existing critical/warning compliance tile"). Two cards in a 3-column grid: (a) **Run-FMCSA-Scan card** (1 col) — Shield icon + section title + 1-line context ("Re-check every approved carrier against FMCSA. Runs daily at 3 AM Eastern automatically; manual re-scan anytime."), gold-token button using existing `bg-gold/20 hover:bg-gold/30 border-gold/40 text-gold` chrome from the codebase, Zap icon when idle / Loader2 when starting; on click POSTs `/integrations/fmcsa/bulk-monitor` and on 202 surfaces inline confirmation message ("Scan started — results in about two minutes.") that auto-clears after 6s; error path shows red inline message + auto-resets after 6s. Button never blocks for the ~2-min scan — fire-and-forget pattern from v3.8.ahy backend half. (b) **Last FMCSA Scan card** (2 cols) — Clock icon + section title + 4-cell stat grid (When / Carriers Scanned / Changes Found / Auto-Suspended). When-cell uses new `formatScanTimestamp` helper for relative time ("just now", "12 min ago", "3 hr ago", or absolute date+time after 24hr). Changes-Found and Auto-Suspended cells turn yellow / red respectively when > 0 for at-a-glance signal. Empty-state copy ("No scans recorded yet. Run a scan or wait for the daily 3 AM ET cron.") for cold-start. **State management:** local component state via `useState` (no global store needed) — `scanState: "idle" | "starting" | "started" | "error"`; `scanMessage: string | null`. Last-scan query uses TanStack `useQuery` with `refetchInterval: scanState === "started" ? 30_000 : false` so the card auto-refreshes during the post-click window without polling forever (also a 180_000ms timeout resets scanState to idle to halt polling regardless). **Styling fidelity:** all classes pulled from existing CeoOverview tokens — `bg-white/5`, `border-white/10`, `rounded-xl`, `p-5`, `font-semibold text-white`, `text-slate-400`, `text-slate-500`, `text-gold`, `text-yellow-400`, `text-red-400`, `text-green-400`. No new brand surfaces, no new icon imports — `Shield`, `Clock`, `Loader2`, `Zap` were all already imported (lines 8-13). Per directive: "Match the existing CeoOverview styling and tokens; no new brand surfaces." **Scope discipline per directive:** "Halt at the boundary — run-button and summary only, not a full scan-history browser." Did not add: scan-history list, per-carrier scan detail expand, ComplianceScan history tab, ComplianceAlert browser. Those remain banked at Item 186. **Pre-commit gates:** frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx next build` clean (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity). Per §3.1: v3.8.ahy → v3.8.ahz. |
| v3.8.ahy | (this commit) | **Eastern timezone on human-facing cron jobs + FMCSA scan run-summary + fire-and-forget manual scan endpoint (Item 188 backend half)** — first of two atomic commits ratified together (frontend half lands in the next commit). **Changes:** (1) [`backend/src/cron/index.ts`](backend/src/cron/index.ts) — convention header block added documenting the timezone split; `{ timezone: "America/New_York" }` passed as third arg to `cron.schedule()` on 7 human-facing schedules: `check-call-reminders` (dispatcher Notifications), `tender-expiry-sweep` (carrier emails via sendTenderExpiredEmail), `health-digest` (admin digest email), `monthly-invoice-reminders` (reminder flag → email send pipeline), `monthly-qp-variance` (sendQpVarianceReport email), `compliance-reminders` (daily reminder emails), `ai-morning-briefing` (admin morning briefing). Internal recompute / cleanup / validation jobs untouched per directive ("Leave the FMCSA scan, the cleanups, and any purely-internal jobs on the UTC default") — FMCSA scan, AI learning cycles, OFAC/ELD/TIN/CSA/VIN sweeps, log purges, sequence-advance (creates drafts only, no auto-send), invoice-aging, weekly-report (SystemLog snapshot, no notify) all stay on host UTC. Container TZ not touched. Net effect: human-facing schedules now read in Eastern (e.g. 5am Eastern compliance reminders, 7am Eastern health digest, 6am Eastern monthly reminders) instead of firing at the prior-evening UTC equivalents. Other jobs unchanged. (2) [`backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts`](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts) — `fmcsaComplianceScan` now writes a SINGLE run-summary `SystemLog` row at the end of each run with `details.{startedAt, completedAt, carriersScanned, passed, failed, alertsCreated, autoSuspended, errors}`. New `suspended: 0` counter in the `results` object, incremented at the two suspend write-sites (authority-revoked branch at ~957, OOS branch at ~1068). Severity set to WARNING when errors > 0, INFO otherwise. **Note on logType:** directive asked for a distinct type "like FMCSA_SCAN_RUN" — but Prisma `LogType` is a closed enum (`API_CALL / ERROR / AUTH / SECURITY / PAYMENT / STATUS_CHANGE / CRON_JOB / INTEGRATION / ALERT`). Rather than expand commit scope to a schema migration for one enum value, used `logType: "CRON_JOB"` + `source: "fmcsa-compliance-scan"` filter — equivalent independent-filterability with no schema churn. Documented inline as a follow-up if any future surface needs enum-level filtering. (3) [`backend/src/routes/externalIntegrations.ts:147-194`](backend/src/routes/externalIntegrations.ts#L147) — `POST /integrations/fmcsa/bulk-monitor` now FIRE-AND-FORGET: calls `fmcsaComplianceScan()` via `void ...catch(...)` and returns `202 Accepted` with body `{ message: "FMCSA compliance scan started", note: "Results in about two minutes — poll GET /integrations/fmcsa/last-scan" }`. AE-facing button never holds the HTTP connection open for the ~2-minute scan. ADMIN/CEO/BROKER/OPERATIONS authz unchanged. (4) Same file — new `GET /integrations/fmcsa/last-scan` endpoint reading the most recent CRON_JOB/fmcsa-compliance-scan SystemLog row, returning `{ found, timestamp, message, severity, ...details }` or `{ found: false }` on cold start. Inherits the same authz gate via the file-level `router.use(authorize(...))`. **Sub-pattern 6 sub-rule c verification before edit:** ran `node -e` smoke against `backend/node_modules/node-cron` to confirm `cron.schedule(expr, fn, { timezone: ... })` accepts the third arg without error — node-cron 4.2.1 signature returned `length: 3` and an options-bearing schedule constructed successfully. **Pre-commit gates:** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean (failed first pass with `LogType` enum error on `"FMCSA_SCAN_RUN"`; corrected to `"CRON_JOB"` per the inline note above; clean second pass). **Sub-pattern 7 grep-regex-completeness check:** classified all 27 cron schedules by reading the full file body (not just title-grep) so the human-facing-vs-internal partition is read-verified, not name-inferred. **Scope:** ~140 LOC net across 3 backend files. Within atomic-commit threshold; did not approach halt. Frontend half (CeoOverview Run-FMCSA-Scan button + last-run card) is the next commit per directive's "land as two atomic commits". Per §3.1: v3.8.ahx → v3.8.ahy (Wasi's parallel v3.8.ahx Caravan Journey commit took the next sequential letter while this work was in progress; bumped accordingly). |

(3) [`backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts:799-822`](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L799) — header block rewritten: dropped the stale "manual re-scan via runDailyMonitor" cross-reference (the route now hits this same function); the v3.8.ahv `TODO(item-183-followup)` dual-suspension-column note REWORDED to reflect actual scope — see "Pattern 6 sub-rule c finding" below. (4) [`backend/src/cron/index.ts:312-317`](backend/src/cron/index.ts#L312) — cron section comment updated to drop the stale runDailyMonitor reference and note both scheduled + on-demand paths now resolve to the same canonical scan. **Suspension gate safety verified pre-deletion:** `complianceCheck` at [`complianceMonitorService.ts:115`](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L115) hard-blocks off `carrier.onboardingStatus === "SUSPENDED"` — it does NOT consult `suspensionReason` / `autoSuspendReason` columns. Deleting the bulk monitor (which set `suspensionReason` + `suspendedAt`) doesn't affect gate correctness. **Pattern 6 sub-rule c FIRE — directive premise correction.** The directive's framing — *"Removing it leaves the canonical autoSuspendReason/autoSuspendedAt as the only suspension convention in active use"* — turned out to be incomplete after Phase A grep. `complianceMonitorService.ts` itself writes the **older** `suspensionReason` + `suspendedAt` convention at **4 other call sites** that were NOT in the bulk monitor: insurance-expiry handler ([line 1424](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L1424)), monthly re-vetting auto-suspend ([line 1553](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L1553)), FMCSA authority-change watcher ([line 1662](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L1662)), and FMCSA safety-rating watcher ([line 1702](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L1702)). These are canonical-service flows, not bulk-monitor flows. Closing the v3.8.ahv `TODO(item-183-followup)` would have silently masked residual drift across the canonical service. Resolution: TODO comment **scope-corrected**, not closed — body now enumerates all 6 writers across both conventions and notes which to pick (recommend `autoSuspendReason` / `autoSuspendedAt` — newer pair, already used by the FMCSA branches in this scan + Sprint 40 OverrideComplianceModal path). The methodology lens (grep before claiming an arbitrary column-pair is "the only convention in use") is the load-bearing outcome; the actual unification sprint is now banked as Item 187. **Phase A read-only findings reported in commit message + Items 185 + 186 below** (timezone of daily scan; admin-UI consumers of ComplianceScan / ComplianceAlert rows). **Pre-commit gates:** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean. Per §3.1: v3.8.ahv → v3.8.ahw. |

(2) `backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts:9,800-813` — `weeklyFmcsaScan → fmcsaComplianceScan` rename (cadence-neutral per directive); section banner updated; doc-comment block added noting daily cadence + dual-suspension-column `TODO(item-183-followup)` for later cleanup (`runDailyMonitor` writes `suspensionReason`/`suspendedAt`, this scan writes `autoSuspendReason`/`autoSuspendedAt` — picking one convention deferred). Prevents future confusion about why a function named `runDailyMonitor` is not actually daily. **Rename sprawl:** 5 hits across 2 files (per Phase A grep `weeklyFmcsaScan`) — within the "couple of files" threshold per directive, so the rename proceeded. **FMCSA-courtesy pacing check:** the canonical scan iterates serially over APPROVED-with-DOT carriers without a per-call delay; `verifyCarrierWithFMCSA` has a 6s timeout per call and a 1-hour in-memory cache (`fmcsaService.ts:15` `CACHE_TTL`). At pre-revenue carrier counts (<50 APPROVED today) the 3am cold-cache scan completes in <2min serial. Even at N=200 APPROVED carriers, ~5-7min total — well within QCMobile's undocumented but generous rate budget (industry convention ~10 req/sec sustained is fine; serial pattern is ~0.5 req/sec). The bulk monitor's batchSize=5 + 1s inter-batch delay can be reintroduced into the canonical scan IF carrier count grows past ~500. Documented inline, no change today. **Scope-out (preserved):** `runDailyMonitor` + its POST route stay in place; the rename of the function-named-daily-but-actually-on-demand was specifically not done per Wasi directive ("leave the name and add a comment"). BASICs/SMS + dedicated OOS endpoints not touched — separate work per directive. **Pre-commit gates:** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean. Per §3.1 sequence-continuous: v3.8.ahu → v3.8.ahv. |

No backend changes. **Note:** this row was not added to §11 at original commit time (the parallel `aim` sprint commit message referenced `ain` but did not add the row); banked retroactively alongside the v3.8.aip strip commit per Wasi's "Update CLAUDE.md" directive. |

| v3.8.aio | 2026-05-23 (this commit) | **Build B2 — manual authority-grant-date entry endpoint + admin UI.** Closes the data-exposure surfaced by the 2026-05-23 carrier classification audit: the FMCSA QCMobile `/carriers/{dot}/authority` endpoint returns current-status fields only (`brokerAuthorityStatus`, `commonAuthorityStatus`, etc.), not the historical GRANT events that `fmcsaService.getCarrierAuthority`'s parser targets. All three currently-APPROVED prod carriers + every future post-cutoff carrier resolve to `authorityGrantedDate = NULL`, causing AUTHORITY_UNVERIFIED past the 24h grace per Item 182 gate at [`complianceMonitorService.ts:147`](backend/src/services/complianceMonitorService.ts#L147) — a hard block at tender time despite real active FMCSA authority. Build B2 gives admins a dedicated, reason-required, audited manual data-correction path. **Backend:** new endpoint `POST /carrier/:id/authority-grant-date` (route [`backend/src/routes/carrier.ts`](backend/src/routes/carrier.ts), controller `setAuthorityGrantDate` in [`backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts`](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts) after `updateCarrier`). Validates `grantDate` parses + not in future. Validates `reason` ≥ 10 chars (matches `overrideBlock` convention). Trims + length-caps reason at 500 chars per sanitizeInput convention (no HTML encoding at write time — React auto-escapes on render). ADMIN/CEO authz only — matches `overrideBlock` + `updateCarrier`. Updates `CarrierProfile.authorityGrantedDate`. Emits `auditTrail` row using generic `UPDATE` action + `changedFields.actionDetail = "AUTHORITY_GRANT_DATE_SET"` per the v3.8.ahy / Item 188 precedent (AuditAction is a closed Prisma enum; AUTHORITY_GRANT_DATE_SET is not a member; no schema migration needed — equivalent independent grep-filterability via the actionDetail key). Audit row carries previousGrantDate + newGrantDate + reason + carrierName. After write, calls canonical `complianceCheck(carrierId)` per directive ("do not duplicate gate logic"); the carrier's new compliance verdict is returned to the UI for immediate surfacing. `getAllCarriers` response shape extended with `authorityGrantedDate` so the admin UI can display the current value. **Frontend** (`frontend/src/app/dashboard/carriers/page.tsx`): `Carrier` interface extended with `authorityGrantedDate: string | null`. New state + mutation hook (`authorityGrantInput`, `authorityGrantReason`, `authorityGrantMessage`). New UI section inside the compliance panel tab (`panelTab === "compliance"`), placed after Document Completeness as a third `bg-gray-100 rounded-lg` card titled "FMCSA Authority Grant Date" — matches existing card style. Current value shown as green pill if set, amber "Not set" pill if null, with an inline explanation of the AUTHORITY_UNVERIFIED consequence when missing. Admin-only form (isAdmin gated client-side; backend is authoritative): date input with `max={today}` (mirrors backend not-in-future rule), reason textarea with required-min-10-chars + 500-char cap + live char counter, save button disabled until both inputs valid + while mutation pending, inline success/error banner that includes the post-write compliance verdict in the success path (e.g. "Carrier now passes the compliance check." / "Compliance verdict: Authority is N months old..."). Non-admins see one-line role-required message instead of the form. **Halt boundary respected:** explicitly NOT extended into `updateCarrier`'s multi-field PATCH per directive (the silent path is wrong shape for a legal-identity-class data write per §3.13). No block-resolution-modal surfacing (optional follow-up not in scope). No automated FMCSA re-point work. No Tier 2/3 fence extensions (banked §13.3 Items 189 + 190 from v3.8.aim). **Pattern 6 sub-pattern 6 fire #6 caught + cleaned via Option α (ff-only pull + letter-at-commit-time):** parallel `v3.8.ain` /onboarding Path 2C landed at commit `e41872d` mid-Build-B2. Local ff-pulled (clean fast-forward), all 3 `v3.8.ain` comment refs in working tree (frontend state block + mutation + UI section) bumped to `v3.8.aio` before commit. Letter sequence `ain` (parallel) → `aio` (this commit) is contiguous. **Sub-rule c fire during build:** first backend `tsc` caught `AuditAction` closed-enum issue (`"AUTHORITY_GRANT_DATE_SET"` not assignable); resolved in-place by switching to `"UPDATE"` + encoding the operation in `changedFields.actionDetail` per the established v3.8.ahy / Item 188 precedent rather than expanding scope with a schema migration. Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity): backend `tsc --noEmit` clean (second pass), frontend `tsc --noEmit` + `npx next build` clean. |

(5) Terms Section 5 line on QP tier fees — rewrote to "Optional per-load Quick Pay is available without requiring a factoring contract; published fees apply per the Caravan Partner Program." Dropped Silver 3% / Gold 2% / Platinum 1% / +2% same-day premium catalog. (6) Terms Section 7 lines on tier-placement + advancement thresholds — rewrote to refer carrier to published program. Dropped Silver/Gold/Platinum placement enumeration + the 12/20/30 loads + 90/120/180-day tenure + 97%/98% on-time threshold catalog + Founding recognition stamp framing. (7) Step 0 comment header (lines 511-520 pre-strip) — updated to drop Card C reference + the M1-M6 / Silver-Gold-Platinum string echoes so the verify-grep gate sees zero in dev-facing comments AND user-facing rendered text. **Out-of-strip-scope mentions retained** (NOT on verify-grep list, NOT named in explicit strip list, all operational/contextual not tier-marketing): success-screen Step 1 "Compass Engine is already running its 35-point check..."; Step 5 Review subtitle "The Compass Engine begins its 35-point check immediately on submit."; Step 2 subtitle "The Compass Engine uses both to match lanes."; Card B checklist "Voided check (for Quick Pay direct deposit setup)" — operational requirement inside protected CHROME card per directive preserve bullet. **Verify grep (post-strip, file-wide):** M1-M6 / Net-30/21/14 / QP percentages [1-5]% / "active lane(s)" / Silver/Gold/Platinum / tier-graduated / tier card / tier placement / tier fee / 12/20/30/75/150/300 loads / 90/120/180-day tenure / 97% / 98% on-time — ALL banned patterns return zero on rendered AND comment surfaces. No tier card renders anywhere on the page. **CHROME UNTOUCHED:** OnboardingNav header + "Carrier Registration" eyebrow strip + "What you'll need" checklist card + 5-step form + v3.8.ain step-header eyebrows + brass-accent step indicators + gold-dark CTA button register + insurance/documents/review panel chrome + success-screen chrome — all preserved exactly as v3.8.ain shipped. Onboarding route bundle shrunk from 16.1 kB → 15.1 kB. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 Phase A audit FIRST (audit-driven strip extended into Terms sections when grep revealed they would fail verify), §3.3 atomic single-file ship + CLAUDE.md docs row, §3.2 content-sweep-verify-rendered-output (the verify-grep gate IS the content sweep, run on source pre-deploy), §19 Sub-pattern 7 grep-completeness gate (initial verify exposed comment-level echoes of banned strings; second pass cleaned comments), §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity verification. |

| v3.8.aiq | 2026-05-23 (this commit) | **OnboardingNav visibility regression fix + canonical wordmark removal.** Closes Wasi-flagged "Can you read anything next to logo ?????????" — the v3.8.ain Path 2C OnboardingNav shipped with two compounding problems: (1) non-canonical text wordmark `<span>Silk Route Logistics</span>` next to the logo (the canonical `_partials/nav.html` is logo-only across all public marketing pages — the span was unilateral drift); (2) the wordmark used Tailwind `text-white` on `bg-[#0A2540]` (arbitrary hex), triggering the §13.3 Item 10 backfire — `[data-mode="light"] .text-white { color: var(--srl-text) !important; }` in [`globals.css:162`](frontend/src/app/globals.css#L162) remaps `.text-white` to the dark body-text color in light mode. Same backfire on 4 other badges I introduced in ain (step indicator completed/active circles + success-screen "1" badge + FMCSA error chip "!"). **Methodology miss acknowledged:** Item 10 was banked in §13.3 PRE-ain. The user-memory `feedback_visual_smoke_before_push.md` is explicit that "for layout/hero/card restructure commits, build-gate is NOT enough; must mentally walk viewport proportions before push." I ran `tsc` + `next build` clean and called the gates done, never opened the rendered page in a viewer. Both pre-existing warnings would have prevented this if applied. **Fix (single atomic, 5 surgical edits):** (1) Removed `<span>Silk Route Logistics</span>` entirely from OnboardingNav — logo-only matches canonical `_partials/nav.html`. (2-5) Step indicator completed circle + active circle + success-screen "1" badge + FMCSA error chip "!" — `text-white` → `text-[#FBF7F0]` (arbitrary hex bypasses the `[data-mode="light"]` override). **Pre-existing `text-white` inside AddressAutocomplete (lines 1298 + 1307) untouched** — its `bg-[#0F1117]` IS in the globals.css override list, so bg + text both swap to light-mode pair (cream bg + dark text) and remain readable. Pre-existing component, not introduced by me, works correctly via the override pair semantic. **Visibility class banked:** brand-canonical text-on-navy is `text-[#FBF7F0]` (`--fg-on-navy` per §2.1), NOT Tailwind `text-white`. The arbitrary hex bypasses the `[data-mode="light"] .text-white !important` override. Going forward, all text on hardcoded-navy / hardcoded-dark-color backgrounds must use the arbitrary cream hex, not the Tailwind white token. Mirrors the rule already implicit in the canonical chrome partials (which use `--cream` CSS var, never `.text-white`). **Scope:** ~4 LOC swap across 4 lines + 5 LOC delete (span + flex container simplification) in `onboarding/page.tsx`. No other surfaces touched. **Pre-commit gates:** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` route 15.1 kB). **Patterns applied:** §3.5 Phase A audit (root-cause the text-white override against globals.css before fixing), §3.2 content sweep verify (file-wide text-white grep before declaring done), §3.3 atomic single-file ship, §13.3 Item 10 acknowledgment, §19 Sub-pattern 8 conditional-render-visual-verification (the missed gate that let ain ship the regression). **Patterns emerged:** methodology lesson — Tailwind `text-white` must be treated as a light-mode-aware utility (its override-list semantics), not a theme-neutral "always white" class. Going-forward rule documented inline in OnboardingNav code + here. |

Now lands the carrier directly on the tier system. **Scope:** ~14 LOC removed (EIN checklist line + EIN input block + EIN review display) + ~1 LOC changed (href anchor) in onboarding/page.tsx. No other surfaces touched. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` shrunk from 15.1 kB to 14.9 kB — the EIN input + subtitle paragraph was ~200 bytes of JSX). **Letter:** aiq is latest origin/main HEAD; air sequence-continuous on top. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first (full grep of "ein\|EIN" across file before removal, found all 5 surfaces, classified state-vs-render); §3.2 content sweep verify (post-removal grep confirmed only form-state defaults remain, no rendered EIN surface left); §3.3 atomic single-file ship + CLAUDE.md docs row; §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity verification. **Patterns emerged:** methodology lesson — Wasi's "is X usually here or does it come later?" audit-style questions are typically REMOVE signals, not explain-it signals. Two consecutive fires on this misinterpretation (Path 2C chrome audit + EIN question both misread as "explain"). | v3.8.ait | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Step 1/2 field-placement IA fix + OnboardingNav Sign In canonical parity.** Closes Wasi-flagged *"# of trucks is showing here on company information already and phone number is showing on top part"* + *"sign in on top left doesn't exactly match the sign in CTA of other pages"* + the surfaced point *"this must have been caught by you instead of meeeee"*. **Methodology miss (third in two days):** v3.8.ain Path 2C chrome upgrade preserved the inherited pre-ain field placement without questioning whether the categorical grouping made operational sense — fleet size (# of Trucks) belongs with equipment/fleet info, not with company identity; Phone belongs with contact details (Email), not with regulatory IDs (DOT/MC). Both should have been caught during the ain visual smoke per `feedback_visual_smoke_before_push.md` which I skipped. **Fix (single atomic, 5 surgical changes):** (1) Step 1 top grid restructured 4-col (DOT/MC/Trucks/Phone) → 2-col (DOT/MC only) — kept as 2 distinct inputs not a dropdown because FMCSA auto-lookup works in both directions; a dropdown would force selecting one type and lose the bidirectional fill UX. (2) Phone * moved from Step 1 top grid → bottom contact block paired with Email as sm:grid-cols-2; Password split into its own full-width row below — grouping: identity → contact → auth. (3) # of Trucks moved Step 1 → Step 2 (Equipment & Operating Regions) as top field max-w-xs single column; relabeled "Fleet Size (# of Trucks)"; Step 2 intro updated to "Tell us your fleet size, what you haul, and where you run." (4) OnboardingNav Sign In button canonical parity: dropped ChevronDown icon (canonical `.nav-login-btn` has no chevron); tightened padding `px-5 py-2` → `py-[9px] px-[22px]` matching utilities.css canonical exactly; removed unused ChevronDown lucide-react import. (5) Eyebrow strip "Already registered? Sign In" link removed entirely — the canonical nav above already provides Sign In via the gold-dark CTA dropdown; the eyebrow strip link was a second Sign In affordance in a different visual register (plain-text underlined vs canonical CTA button), creating visual inconsistency AND non-canonical drift (no other public marketing page has an in-body "Already registered?" prompt below the nav). Eyebrow now carries only the page-context cue (program eyebrow + Carrier Registration H1). **Methodology acknowledgment:** Three consecutive sprints have shipped layout-audit gaps Wasi caught post-deploy: ail (EIN should-have-been-removed misread as "explain"), ain (text-white invisible + non-canonical wordmark), ait (Trucks/Phone wrong-category placement + mismatched Sign In CTA). Going forward: must run a step-by-step IA + visual walkthrough of every chrome/layout sprint before declaring gates passed. Build-clean is necessary, not sufficient. **Scope:** ~25 LOC restructured across 4 surfaces in onboarding/page.tsx. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` 15.0 kB → 14.9 kB). **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first; §3.3 atomic single-file ship + CLAUDE.md docs row; §3.2 visual smoke walkthrough pre-push (applied this time, missed in the prior three sprints); §19 Pattern 7 design-system conformance. **Patterns emerged:** methodology lesson — chrome-polish sprints that touch existing layouts MUST include an IA audit, not just a styling audit. Pre-ain layout grouped fields by tab-order convenience but the categorical grouping was wrong. Banked as clarification of `feedback_visual_smoke_before_push` — visual smoke includes IA review, not just pixel-level layout. |

| v3.8.aiu | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Address auto-fill from FMCSA + Email/Password browser autofill opt-out.** Closes two Wasi-flagged onboarding bugs on deployed v3.8.ait: (1) when entering DOT or MC, the Address field stayed empty despite FMCSA returning a populated address (City/State/Zip auto-populated correctly); (2) Email and Password fields were pre-filled by Chrome browser autofill before the user typed. **Fix 1 — AddressAutocomplete bidirectional sync:** the helper component at line 1232+ had a one-way useEffect at line 1262 — `if (!value) setQuery("")` — which only cleared the input when value became empty, NOT when a new non-empty value arrived from the parent (e.g. FMCSA auto-fill via applyFmcsaData → set("address", data.phyStreet)). The prop updated but the input's internal `query` state stayed empty, so the field appeared blank. Rewrote to `useEffect(() => { setQuery(value || ""); }, [value]);` for bidirectional sync. React short-circuits if value === query so the user-type path (handleChange → setQuery → onChange → parent → prop update → effect fires → setQuery(same)) doesn't cause infinite re-renders. **Fix 2 — Browser autofill opt-out on Email + Phone + Password:** applied the existing EIN-field opt-out pattern (autoComplete + non-standard `name`) to all three contact-block inputs. Email: `autoComplete="off"` + `name="carrier-registration-email"`. Phone: `autoComplete="off"` + `name="carrier-registration-phone"`. Password: `autoComplete="new-password"` (W3C-recommended for new-account password fields — signals browser not to suggest existing passwords) + `name="carrier-registration-password"`. Non-standard `name` values avoid Chrome's pattern-matching against common field names (email/username/password) that often overrides `autoComplete="off"`. **Scope:** ~5 LOC changed in onboarding/page.tsx (1 useEffect rewrite + 3 input attribute additions). No other surfaces touched. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` 14.9 kB, no change). **Letter:** ait latest origin/main HEAD; aiu sequence-continuous on top. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first (read AddressAutocomplete value-prop sync logic + Email/Password input attributes before fixing — the bug was in the useEffect dependency-handling, NOT in the applyFmcsaData callback); §3.3 atomic single-file ship + CLAUDE.md docs row; §3.2 visual smoke walkthrough pre-push (mentally walked the auto-fill flow start-to-finish + browser-autofill opt-out semantics for both fixes); §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity verification. **Patterns emerged:** none new — both bugs are well-known React patterns (prop-to-state sync + browser autofill opt-out). Banking the AddressAutocomplete sync fix as a reference for any other prop-driven uncontrolled-input components that may have the same one-way clear-only effect pattern. |

| v3.8.aiw | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Sprint B — COI verification via Effective + Expiration date pair (full-stack atomic).** Closes the operational gap Wasi flagged mid-aiv: pre-aiw each of the 4 insurance policies on Step 3 captured a single date (expiration only) — could verify "policy not expired yet" but NOT "policy currently in force." A pre-issued policy with future effective date would be wrongly accepted as active. Industry-standard COI verification requires both: `effective <= today <= expiry` → ACTIVE; `today < effective` → NOT YET ACTIVE; `today > expiry` → EXPIRED. **Backend changes:** (1) `prisma/schema.prisma` — 4 new nullable `DateTime?` fields paired with existing `*Expiry` on `CarrierProfile` model (`autoLiabilityEffective`, `cargoInsuranceEffective`, `generalLiabilityEffective`, `workersCompEffective`). (2) New migration file `prisma/migrations/20260524063259_add_insurance_effective_dates/migration.sql` — `ALTER TABLE carrier_profiles ADD` 4 nullable `TIMESTAMP(3)` columns. **Manually authored per §2.2** (avoid `prisma migrate dev` against prod-pointed `DATABASE_URL`); Render's `prisma migrate deploy` applies on next push. (3) `src/validators/carrier.ts` — 4 new `z.string().optional()` entries. (4) `src/controllers/carrierController.ts` — `registerCarrier` writer adds 4 new Date conversions matching the existing `*Expiry` pattern; `updateCarrier` writer destructures + conditionally writes 4 new fields. **Frontend changes (`onboarding/page.tsx`):** (1) `InsuranceLineData` interface: `effective: string` added alongside existing `expiry: string`. (2) `emptyInsLine` const: `effective: ""` added. (3) Step 3 UI: each of the 4 insurance rows restructured from a single 4-col grid → 2-row layout (Row 1 `sm:grid-cols-3`: Provider / Policy # / Coverage Amount; Row 2 `sm:grid-cols-2`: Effective Date / Expiration Date). Cleaner than 5-col cramped layout; matches "identity fields above scheduling dates" visual hierarchy. (4) `handleSubmit` payload extended with 4 new `*Effective` fields. (5) Step 5 Review Insurance Summary shows both dates per policy. **Schema mutation discipline (§2.2):** migration file authored manually rather than `prisma migrate dev` because backend/.env has prod Neon URL — running migrate dev against prod is forbidden. Render's canonical build chain (`prisma migrate deploy` + `prisma migrate status --exit-code`) applies + verifies on next push. **Scope:** ~250 LOC across 5 files. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean (Prisma client regenerated via `npx prisma generate`); frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean; frontend `npx next build` clean (`/onboarding` 15.4 kB → 15.6 kB, expected ~0.2 kB from 4 new date inputs + Review echo). **Letter:** aiv latest origin/main HEAD; aiw sequence-continuous on top. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first (banked Effective Date item in aiv close as Sprint B candidate, then executed end-to-end this sprint); §3.3 atomic full-stack ship + CLAUDE.md docs row + 1 Prisma migration file; §2.2 schema mutation discipline (manual migration authoring); §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity verification (both frontend AND backend tsc gates run). **Patterns emerged:** none new. **Post-deploy verification required:** on Render auto-deploy after push — (1) `prisma migrate deploy` should apply the new migration (~1s ALTER TABLE); (2) `prisma migrate status --exit-code` post-gate should pass; (3) Smoke test: register a test carrier with both Effective + Expiry dates per policy, verify `CarrierProfile` row has the new columns populated via SQL. |

| v3.8.aix | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Phone formatter + γ "Very Strong" password tier (14 chars + 4 char classes + HIBP breach check) + confirm-password + live strength meter.** Two Wasi-flagged items shipped as a single full-stack atomic. **(1) Phone formatter:** new `formatPhone(raw)` utility strips non-digits, drops leading "1" country code on paste, caps at 10 digits, formats progressively as `(XXX) XXX-XXXX` on every keystroke. Applied to Step 1 Phone input + Step 3 Insurance Agent Phone input. `canNext` digit-count check via regex instead of total char length. **(2) Password γ tier (Very Strong):** new helpers `passwordCriteria(pw)` (returns 5 booleans), `passwordMeetsCriteria(pw)`, `checkPasswordPwned(pw)` (SHA-1 via `crypto.subtle.digest` + HIBP k-anonymity `api.pwnedpasswords.com/range/{5-char-prefix}`), `passwordStrength(pw, hibpStatus)` (returns `null` / `WEAK` / `STRONG` / `VERY_STRONG`). New form state: `confirmPassword`, `hibpStatus`, `hibpCount`, `hibpTimer`. Debounced 600ms useEffect watches `form.password` — resets HIBP to "unknown" until composition met, then fires async check. **Password UI replacement:** 2-col layout — Left: Password + Confirm Password inputs (Confirm border/bg react to match state, inline "✓ match" / "✗ don't match" cue); Right: persistent Requirements card (cream panel + cream-2 border + gold-dark eyebrow + 5-row checklist with ✓ filled green circles when met, empty cream-2 circles when not + 3-segment strength meter bar + "Strength: Weak | Strong | Very Strong" text label + HIBP status line). `canNext` requires (a) all 5 composition criteria, (b) `confirmPassword === password`, (c) `hibpStatus === "safe"`. "Checking" / "unknown" / "error" / "pwned" all block progression. **Backend:** `backend/src/validators/carrier.ts` Zod chain extended — `z.string().min(14).regex(/[A-Z]/).regex(/[a-z]/).regex(/[0-9]/).regex(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/)`. Defense-in-depth: even if frontend bypassed, backend rejects weak passwords. HIBP not re-checked server-side (frontend handles it). **CSP:** `frontend/public/_headers` `connect-src` extended with `https://api.pwnedpasswords.com` — HIBP k-anonymity API requires direct browser fetch. Without this CSP allowance the fetch would be blocked + `checkPasswordPwned` would throw, setting `hibpStatus="error"` and blocking `canNext` indefinitely. **Scope:** ~280 LOC across 4 files. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` 15.6 kB → 17 kB, expected ~1.4 kB from password block + helpers); backend tsc --noEmit clean. **Letter:** aiw latest origin/main HEAD; aix sequence-continuous on top. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first (paused mid-sprint to clarify password tier with user before shipping spec γ vs β — industry-standard reference framework table comparison faster than ship + re-ship); §3.3 atomic full-stack ship (frontend + backend + CSP + docs); §3.2 visual smoke walkthrough pre-push; §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity verification (both frontend AND backend tsc gates run). **Patterns emerged:** methodology lesson — when user proposes a security-class spec with a strength label ("strong"), pause and validate against industry-standard tiering (NIST SP 800-63B, OWASP) BEFORE shipping. The "good vs strong vs very strong" classification has explicit reference frameworks; asking the user "which tier" with a comparison table is faster than shipping and re-shipping. Banked as feedback-loop optimization. **Post-deploy verification required:** on Cloudflare + Render rebuild — (1) phone formats live `(XXX) XXX-XXXX`, caps at 10 digits; (2) typing `Password123!` (12 chars) → WEAK; (3) typing `Password1234!!` (14 chars, all classes) → STRONG → HIBP fires → "Appears in N breaches" → can't proceed; (4) unique strong password → VERY_STRONG → Next enables; (5) Confirm mismatch shows red border + blocking message. |

| v3.8.alh | 2026-05-27 | **v3.8.ald test-mock regression fix — CI back to 224/224 green after 3 commits red on GitHub Actions backend job (Sub-pattern 11 third fire + npm test added to canonical pre-commit gate).** Pre-alh state: GitHub Actions CI had been failing on the Backend - Lint, Test & Build job across the v3.8.alf push (2026-05-26) and v3.8.alg push (2026-05-27). Root cause traced via `gh run view --log-failed` to 10 test failures all in `__tests__/unit/controllers/authController.test.ts`, all with the same TypeError shape: `prisma.user.findFirst is not a function`. The diagnostic chain: `__tests__/setup.ts:6-11` defined the `prisma.user` mock with `findUnique + findMany + create + update` but NOT `findFirst`. v3.8.ald swapped 5 callsites in `authController.ts` from `findUnique` → `findFirst` (lines 126 register-dupe + 169 login + 258 handleVerifyOtp + 374 handleResendOtp + 584 resetPassword) for case-insensitive email lookup via `caseInsensitiveEmailFilter`. Mock lacking the method → TypeError on every test exercising those paths. Production unaffected — Render's build chain runs npm install + tsc + check-direct-url + prisma migrate deploy/status + cp -r, but NOT vitest. Two surfaces, two gates, three commits where they diverged. **v3.8.alh fix shape:** (1) added `findFirst: vi.fn()` to setup.ts user mock (matches the canonical pattern used by every other model — otpCode/shipment/invoice/loadTender/carrierProfile/customer all carry both `findUnique` and `findFirst`). Single LOC. (2) First-wave validation: 10 failures → 6 failures (TypeError class cleared; 6 happy-path tests now read undefined from bare `vi.fn()` and assert against expected success → fail). (3) Re-pointed 6 test mocks from `findUnique.mockResolvedValue(...)` → `findFirst.mockResolvedValue(...)` at the 5 ald-modified call paths' test setups in authController.test.ts (`register > returns 409 for duplicate email` at line 66, `login > sends OTP for valid credentials` at line 86, `handleVerifyOtp > returns token on valid OTP` at line 157, `handleVerifyOtp > returns passwordExpired for expired password` at line 198, `handleResendOtp > sends a new code on success` at line 241, `handleResendOtp > returns 429 when rate limited` at line 269). Did NOT alias `findFirst` to `findUnique` in the shared mock per directive — that would hide exactly this class of divergence. (4) Second-wave validation: 6 failures → 1 failure. The 1 remaining at `handleResendOtp > returns generic message for unknown user` was mock-state-leakage subtlety — vitest's `clearAllMocks` in beforeEach clears call history but NOT `mockResolvedValue` settings, so the truthy user from the prior "returns 429" test (line 269 setup) leaked into the next test's `findFirst` resolved value, causing the controller to skip the "no user" branch and hit the rate-limit branch. Fix: explicitly `mockPrisma.user.findFirst.mockResolvedValue(null)` at line 286. (5) Final validation: **224 / 224 tests passing across 28 test files in 3.97s**. Did not surface anything beyond the mock/test-setup class — no genuine controller logic failures, no Prisma client behavioral changes, no environment drift. **Methodology bankings:** (a) **Sub-pattern 11 third fire** — Sprint 51 (JSX escape) + v3.8.ajg (env() missing in CI workflow) + v3.8.ald (`findFirst` mock gap). Three distinct operational contexts where the CI-parity-verification mechanic recurred — ESLint, Prisma config, vitest mock. Three-fire validation lineage now achieved (Sub-pattern 11 was already canonical at origin per Item 148; three fires demonstrate the breadth of contexts). **Sub-pattern 11 case study #3 added to §19 entry below the existing case studies #1 + #2.** (b) **npm test added to canonical pre-commit gate at CLAUDE.md §3.3** — gate now reads `npx tsc --noEmit` from backend + `npm test` from backend + `npx next build` from frontend must all pass clean. Local verify cost +~5s per sprint; CI hotfix cycle cost avoided. (c) **Banked observation — Render-vs-CI gate divergence:** Render's build chain doesn't run vitest. A passing Render deploy is NOT evidence of a passing test suite. Production stayed green across ald/alf/alg pushes while CI was red because the failure class (mock setup gap) doesn't fire at runtime — real Prisma client has `findFirst`, only the mock didn't. Future class of "passing build" status that doesn't reflect full test suite — diagnose CI-only failures BEFORE assuming production impact. **Sub-rule c registry advances 32 → 33** — every authoritative-source check on the failure shape was load-bearing for diagnosing this in <5 min without a blind fix (CI log → exact failing tests → exact mock definition → exact controller call site). **Files: 4** (backend/__tests__/setup.ts +1/0 LOC user mock + findFirst, backend/__tests__/unit/controllers/authController.test.ts ~17 LOC swap across 7 mock setups, frontend/src/components/ui/VersionFooter.tsx version bump + 17-line marker, CLAUDE.md §3.3 pre-commit gate update + §11 row + §19 Sub-pattern 11 case study #3). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11 (now extended):** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean, **backend `npm test` clean (224/224)**, frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean + `npx next build` clean (no frontend source changes; gates run defensively). No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new package install. Per §3.1: v3.8.alg → v3.8.alh sequence-continuous. **Push posture:** Render deploy will be a no-op for production — `__tests__/` directory + setup.ts are not part of the deployed artifact (tsc compiles only `src/`). CI will run npm test on push and turn green. **Letter:** alg latest origin/main HEAD; alh sequence-continuous. |
