| v3.8.ale | 2026-05-26 | **Email DB-level case-insensitive uniqueness via citext (closes the auth-ambiguity v3.8.ald leaned on).** Pre-ale: v3.8.ald's `findFirst({ where: caseInsensitiveEmailFilter(email) })` assumed exactly one user matched per email case-insensitively, but nothing at the DB level enforced that uniqueness — the original `email String @unique` constraint was case-sensitive Postgres B-tree, so two concurrent registrations of `Test@x.com` + `test@x.com` could both pass the application-level findFirst (returns null), both insert, the @unique B-tree accepts both as distinct, then ald's findFirst becomes nondeterministic on subsequent lookups (Prisma picks one of N matching rows). Also: read-path performance — `mode: "insensitive"` compiles to ILIKE, which Postgres planner doesn't reliably substitute the B-tree index for; sequential scan at scale. **Phase A dedupe scan (mandatory pre-migration gate):** new [`backend/scripts/dedupe-scan-email.ts`](backend/scripts/dedupe-scan-email.ts) ran against Neon prod via runtime Prisma client. Query `SELECT LOWER(TRIM(email)) AS e, COUNT(*) FROM users GROUP BY 1 HAVING COUNT(*) > 1`. **Result: CLEAN — 0 case-duplicate groups across 10 users + 4 carrier profiles + 3 known test-carrier MC#s (matches v3.8.aim baseline).** Connection host verified `ep-green-frog-ajsgv9me-pooler.c-3.us-east-2.aws.neon.tech` (prod). Safe to apply additively. **Path β — citext column conversion** (vs Path α functional UNIQUE INDEX on lower(email)). Cleaner shape because: (a) Path α requires queries to use `WHERE LOWER(email) = $1` to hit the functional index — Prisma's mode: "insensitive" compiles to ILIKE which the planner doesn't reliably rewrite. (b) Path α leaves TWO unique constraints coexisting (original case-sensitive + new case-insensitive). (c) Path β moves case-insensitivity to the column's equality operator, so the EXISTING `users_email_key` UNIQUE INDEX automatically rebuilds with citext_ops (operator class shifts text_ops → citext_ops, no new index needed, no redundancy). Also: Prisma's `findUnique({ where: { email } })` now works case-insensitively without `mode: "insensitive"` — ald's mode-insensitive filter becomes redundant but harmless. **Migration shape (2 SQL statements):** (1) `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS citext;` — Neon supports citext as a standard contrib extension. (2) `ALTER TABLE users ALTER COLUMN email TYPE citext;` — Postgres preserves the underlying B-tree index by rebuilding it with citext_ops; identity-preserving conversion on already-citext data is a metadata-only op. **Apply path — DIRECT against Neon, NOT via Render:** per directive ("do NOT rely on the Render build chain to have applied it, per the open §13.3 Item 8.10 investigation"). [`backend/scripts/apply-email-citext.ts`](backend/scripts/apply-email-citext.ts) uses runtime Prisma client `$executeRawUnsafe` through the pooled `DATABASE_URL` (only env var set in local backend/.env — `DIRECT_URL` not yet set, so prisma migrate CLI was blocked by the v3.8.ajg `directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")` schema datasource line). Raw exec bypasses the migrate CLI entirely; ALTER TABLE on a 10-row table completed in <100ms. Migration file at [`prisma/migrations/20260526164943_email_citext/migration.sql`](backend/prisma/migrations/20260526164943_email_citext/migration.sql) ships alongside this commit; Render's next deploy will idempotently re-apply (`CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS` + `ALTER COLUMN TYPE citext` on already-citext = both no-ops) + ledger-record in `_prisma_migrations`. **Verification (5 checks, all PASSED on Neon):** [`backend/scripts/verify-email-citext.ts`](backend/scripts/verify-email-citext.ts) confirms — (a) `pg_extension WHERE extname='citext'` → citext v1.6 enabled; (b) `information_schema.columns` → users.email udt_name=citext; (c) `pg_indexes WHERE indexname='users_email_key'` → exists on citext column (B-tree on citext uses citext_ops by default for the index's equality operator); (d) behavioral probe `SELECT ('Test@Example.com'::citext = 'test@example.com'::citext)` → TRUE; (e) live round-trip — Prisma `findUnique({ where: { email: "DISPATCH@SILKROUTELOGISTICS.AI" } })` resolved a row stored as `dispatch@silkroutelogistics.ai`. Behavioral confirmation that the post-ald code path (and any future findUnique-on-email caller) now works case-insensitively at the DB layer without any application-level filter. **Schema.prisma updated:** `email String @unique` → `email String @unique @db.Citext` so future `prisma migrate diff` and `prisma db pull` operations stay in sync. **Architectural pattern banked:** when application-layer uniqueness assumptions (ald's findFirst returning ≤1 row) can be backed by a DB-level constraint cheaply, do it — closes the race window + the planner uncertainty in one move. Citext is the canonical Postgres shape for case-insensitive email; precedent set here for similar future moves (e.g. case-insensitive MC#/DOT# normalization, if/when those land). **Pattern 6 sub-rule c fire #32 banked:** initial assumption was that ` UNIQUE INDEX ON LOWER(email)` would back the v3.8.ald `mode: "insensitive"` queries cleanly. Authoritative-source check on Prisma's query compilation (mode: "insensitive" → ILIKE, not LOWER(...)=...) + Postgres planner behavior on ILIKE-vs-functional-index disproved that. Pivoted to citext before authoring the migration. Cumulative §19 sub-rule c registry advances 31 → 32. **Banked for future sprints:** (a) Phone DB-uniqueness — explicitly NOT in v3.8.ale per directive scope. Needs the indexed `phoneNormalized` column refactor first (banked at ~10K+ rows threshold since v3.8.ala); race-closure not auth-ambiguity, so lower urgency. (b) Schema-level `.transform(v => v.toLowerCase())` on registerSchema + loginSchema — still banked, but citext-on-DB now means the schema transforms are belt-and-suspenders not load-bearing. (c) Add `DIRECT_URL` to local backend/.env per §2.2 canonical so future `prisma migrate` CLI operations don't require the `$executeRawUnsafe` workaround — surfaced as gap during this sprint's apply step. (d) `prisma migrate resolve --applied 20260526164943_email_citext` ledger-record can be run manually post-DIRECT_URL-fix if Wasi wants the ledger consistent before Render's next deploy ledger-records it. (e) OTP / SMS verification still gated on SMS-path decision. **Files: 7** (new `backend/prisma/migrations/20260526164943_email_citext/migration.sql`, `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` +6/-1 LOC @db.Citext annotation, NEW `backend/scripts/dedupe-scan-email.ts` ~50 LOC, NEW `backend/scripts/apply-email-citext.ts` ~30 LOC, NEW `backend/scripts/verify-email-citext.ts` ~90 LOC, `backend/scripts/verify-db-target.ts` sanity-check script ~25 LOC, VersionFooter.tsx version bump + 10-line marker, CLAUDE.md §11 row). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** prisma generate clean (Prisma client regenerated with citext type), backend tsc --noEmit clean, frontend tsc --noEmit + npx next build clean. Per §3.1: v3.8.ald → v3.8.ale sequence-continuous. **Letter:** ald latest origin/main HEAD; ale sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ald | 2026-05-26 | **Email read-path case-insensitivity across all 8 lookup sites (closes v3.8.alb half-fix).** Pre-ald: v3.8.alb canonicalized email to lowercase on the carrier registration WRITE site at [carrierController.ts:99](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts#L99) — but the 7 OTHER email-keyed lookup sites stayed case-sensitive. A carrier stored as `test@example.com` (post-alb new registrations always lowercase) could log in as `test@example.com` but NOT as `Test@Example.com` — `prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } })` against case-sensitive Postgres collation returns null. UX bypass: carrier types email with any capitalization variant they used during signup or out of habit and login silently fails with "Invalid credentials". Compounded for pre-alb mixed-case stored rows (`Test@Example.com` stored as-typed, login normalizes nothing → never resolves). Phase A audit confirmed 8 lookup sites total (not the 6 estimated in v3.8.alb): [authController.ts:123/165/253/371/581](backend/src/controllers/authController.ts) (AE-register-dupe + login + handleVerifyOtp + forgotPassword + resetPassword), [routes/auth.ts:82](backend/src/routes/auth.ts#L82) (admin token mint — already pre-lowercased on line 77 but case-sensitive findUnique misses pre-existing mixed-case), [routes/carrierAuth.ts:95/442](backend/src/routes/carrierAuth.ts) (carrier login + resend-otp). carrierController.ts:112 was already fixed in v3.8.alb. **v3.8.ald shape — new shared helper file + apply to all 8 sites:** NEW [`backend/src/lib/emailNormalization.ts`](backend/src/lib/emailNormalization.ts) mirrors `phoneNormalization.ts` (~50 LOC w/ docs): (a) `normalizeEmail(raw): string | null` — lowercase + trim, null on invalid input; used at the carrier registration WRITE site; (b) `caseInsensitiveEmailFilter(email): { email: { equals, mode: "insensitive" } }` — returns a Prisma where-clause selector reusable across all 8 read-path sites. **Carrier registration write site** at carrierController.ts:99 replaces the alb-era inline `data.email = data.email.toLowerCase().trim()` with `const normalizedEmail = normalizeEmail(data.email); if (!normalizedEmail) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid email" }); return; } data.email = normalizedEmail;` — single source of truth, defensive null-check. **8 read-path sites** swap from `findUnique({ where: { email } })` → `findFirst({ where: caseInsensitiveEmailFilter(email) })`. carrierAuth.ts:95 also carries the existing `include: { carrierProfile: true }` through the switch (findFirst supports the same include syntax). **Performance note for §13.3 banking:** Prisma's `mode: "insensitive"` compiles to Postgres ILIKE under the hood — bypasses the `User.email @unique` B-tree index. Acceptable at pre-revenue volumes (<1000 users); the citext / `lower(email)` unique-index migration is the proper performance fix at scale, already banked since v3.8.alb. **Architectural pattern banked (consistent with the v3.8.akz unified-gate convention + v3.8.ala phoneNormalization mirror):** when a domain primitive needs both canonical-write + case/format-insensitive-read, the helpers live as a paired set in `backend/src/lib/<primitive>Normalization.ts`. emailNormalization is the second member of this lib (after phoneNormalization). Future primitives (e.g. address normalization for the chameleon fingerprint refactor, MC#/DOT# canonical formatting) inherit this pattern. **Out of scope (banked):** (a) AE/shipper register WRITE-side `normalizeEmail` (authController.ts has no `prisma.user.create` write-side normalization today; AE/shipper registrations still store email as-typed — but the duplicate-check read at line 123 is now case-insensitive, closing the v3.8.alb-style bypass on that path). Banked for a future register-write-symmetry sprint. (b) `lower(email)` unique-index / citext column migration — directive migration-free. (c) Schema-side `.transform(v => v.toLowerCase())` on registerSchema + loginSchema — would also work but the lib-helper approach centralizes the contract; banked alongside the citext refactor. (d) OTP / SMS verification — still gated on Wasi's SMS-path decision. **Files: 6** (NEW `backend/src/lib/emailNormalization.ts`, carrierController.ts ~6 LOC net replacing alb's inline, authController.ts 5-site swap + 1 import, routes/auth.ts 1-site swap + 1 import, routes/carrierAuth.ts 2-site swap + 1 import, VersionFooter.tsx version bump + 9-line marker, CLAUDE.md §11 row). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend tsc --noEmit clean, frontend tsc --noEmit + npx next build clean. No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new package install. Per §3.1: v3.8.alc → v3.8.ald sequence-continuous. **Letter:** alc latest origin/main HEAD; ald sequence-continuous. Closes the v3.8.alb half-fix that left 7 of 8 email-keyed lookups case-sensitive. |

| v3.8.alc | 2026-05-26 | **Required-doc gate hardened against client-asserted spoofing (closes v3.8.alb regression).** Pre-alc: the v3.8.alb backend required-doc gate at [carrierController.ts:182](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts#L182) read `req.body.docTypes[]` directly — client-asserted, never cross-referenced with the multer file payload. A direct caller sending `docTypes: ["w9","insurance","authority","wc"]` with zero `files` fields passed the gate; the downstream upload loop (correctly file-driven at line 319) then created zero Document rows + the carrier account got created. Gate did NOT close the API-bypass it was built for. Phase A audit confirmed the spoof shape verbatim. **v3.8.alc fix:** derive `presentDocTypes` from `req.files.files` (multer-staged Express.Multer.File[]) — a tag only counts as "present" if the file at the same index actually exists. Specifically: iterate `i` from 0 to `uploadedFiles.length-1` (NOT `clientTags.length-1`), pull `clientTags[i]` only if there's a corresponding file at index i. Tags claimed without files don't add to the present-set. Defense shape: (a) 0 files + N client tags → loop body skipped → presentDocTypes empty → all required missing → 422; (b) 1 file + 4 client tags → loop runs once → presentDocTypes = {first tag} → 3 missing → 422; (c) 4 files + 4 client tags (valid) → all 4 added → pass. Same 422 response shape (`{ error: "Missing required documents", missing: [...] }`) and same Canadian Safety Cert conditional. **Architectural pattern banked:** server-side validation must derive presence from server-controlled inputs (multer files, request metadata) — NEVER from client-asserted parallel arrays. Pairs with the v3.8.ala / alb "compare normalized" principle: when client-supplied inputs feed validation, the validation must normalize/derive a server-canonical view before deciding. Future sprints adding any document-class gate (e.g. v3.8.aky's banked "schema-level docTypes validation in carrierRegisterSchema") inherit this — schema validation of the array is necessary-but-not-sufficient; gate must read files. **Out of scope (banked):** (a) MIME-type / file-signature validation per-document — gate is about PRESENCE, not content. (b) Schema-side `.superRefine()` on `carrierRegisterSchema` requires declaring `docTypes` + `files` in the schema first (currently both absent); would add ~25 LOC. Controller-side fix is the minimal-surface close. (c) Mirror this server-derivation pattern at the AE/shipper register path — that path has no required-doc gate today, so no spoof vector exists yet; banked if/when the AE/shipper register adopts doc requirements. (d) email read-path normalization across the 8 lookup sites → next commit v3.8.ald. **Files: 3** (carrierController.ts ~7 LOC net source change replacing the client-asserted check with file-derived check, VersionFooter.tsx version bump + 5-line marker, CLAUDE.md §11 row). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend tsc --noEmit clean, frontend tsc --noEmit + npx next build clean (no frontend source changes; gates run defensively). No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new package install. Per §3.1: v3.8.alb → v3.8.alc sequence-continuous. **Letter:** alb latest origin/main HEAD; alc sequence-continuous. v3.8.ald next for the email read-path work. |

| v3.8.alb | 2026-05-26 | **Registration-endpoint hardening — two defense-in-depth gaps, single atomic, migration-free.** Closes (A) a live case-sensitivity bypass shipped in v3.8.ala's email duplicate-check, and (C) the pre-existing backend required-doc gate gap surfaced by the v3.8.aky audit. **Item A — email case-insensitivity.** Pre-alb: `registerCarrier` at [carrierController.ts:108](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts#L108) did `prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: data.email } })` — Postgres default collation makes this case-sensitive, so `Test@example.com` slipped past an existing `test@example.com` and defeated the duplicate check. Stored as-typed at line 173. The `@unique` constraint at schema.prisma:701 didn't help — Postgres unique B-tree on case-sensitive collation allows mixed-case duplicates as distinct strings. v3.8.alb fix: (1) `data.email = data.email.toLowerCase().trim()` immediately after the Zod parse — all new registrations land in canonical lowercase form. (2) Duplicate lookup swapped from `findUnique` to `findFirst({ where: { email: { equals: data.email, mode: "insensitive" } } })` — belt-and-suspenders against any pre-existing mixed-case stored row (~0 expected at pre-revenue volume but cheap to be defensive). Storage now consistently lowercase going forward; race-window between concurrent registrations also tightens because the @unique B-tree collision now fires on the lowercased value. **Item C — backend required-doc gate (defense-in-depth).** Pre-alb: per the v3.8.aky audit, `carrierRegisterSchema` had NO `docTypes` / `files` fields and no `.refine()` — frontend canNext step===2 gate (W-9 + COI + Authority + Workers' Comp + Safety if Canadian) was the ONLY enforcement. An attacker hitting `POST /api/carrier/register` directly with curl and omitting the `files` + `docTypes` multipart fields would succeed in creating an unverifiable carrier account. The doc-upload loop at carrierController.ts:285+ just skipped missing tags. v3.8.alb fix: controller-side check inserted between the MC duplicate check and the bcrypt/create block. Reads `req.body.docTypes[]` (already array-normalized by the multer-side middleware at routes/carrier.ts:115), lowercases each entry, builds `requiredDocs = ["w9", "insurance", "authority", "wc", ...(hasCanadianOps ? ["safety"] : [])]` mirroring the frontend canNext exactly. Conditional `hasCanadianOps` reads `data.operatingRegions` and checks for any of the 4 Canadian region strings (`Eastern Canada` / `Western Canada` / `Central Canada` / `Cross-Border`). Returns `422 { error: "Missing required documents", missing: ["w9", "wc"] }` if any required key absent from `submittedDocTypes`. **Enumeration-hygiene note:** unlike v3.8.ala's collision-detection responses (where echoing other carriers' identity would be a leak), the missing-docs response lists the SUBMITTER's own missing docs — operationally useful for legitimate clients debugging a flaky upload, no third-party identity exposure. **Schema-side path rejected** — `.superRefine()` on `carrierRegisterSchema` would also require declaring `docTypes` + `files` in the schema (since they're currently absent), adding ~25 LOC vs the controller-side check's ~10 LOC. Controller-side mirrors the existing duplicate-check pattern. **Scope per directive:** registration endpoint ONLY. Existing carriers are past registration and never re-hit `/register`, so no backfill required and no pre-existing-carrier blast radius. Per-document validation depth (file size / MIME / signature scanning) is OUT of scope — that's the upload-time validation layer at `routes/carrier.ts:115`, intentionally separate. **Banked for future sprints:** (a) Lowercase normalization at the 6 other email-keyed lookup sites (`authController.login`, `authController.handleVerifyOtp`, `authController.forgotPassword`, `authController.resetPassword`, `carrierAuth.login`) — same root case-sensitivity class as Item A but pre-existing UX issue, not v3.8.ala bypass scope. (b) DB-level case-insensitive uniqueness via `citext` extension OR `lower(email)` unique index — would require migration; directive explicitly migration-free. (c) Schema-level `docTypes` validation in `carrierRegisterSchema` for symmetry — banked alongside (b). (d) OTP / SMS verification path — still gated on Wasi's SMS-path decision. **Files: 3** (carrierController.ts ~25 LOC net source change including Item A + Item C, VersionFooter.tsx version bump + 6-line marker comment, CLAUDE.md §11 row). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend tsc --noEmit clean, frontend tsc --noEmit + npx next build clean. No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new package install. Per §3.1: v3.8.ala → v3.8.alb sequence-continuous. **Sprint discipline applied from prior turn feedback** ("Keep §11 sprint narrative in CLAUDE.md, not inline code comments — that's most of your 345-vs-150 LOC gap this sprint"): inline code comments kept to 1-2 lines per edit with file-reference + sprint-tag; full narrative lives here in §11 and the commit message. **Letter:** ala latest origin/main HEAD; alb sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ala | 2026-05-26 | **Onboarding contact-security — phone duplicate detection + per-IP + per-contact rate limiting on `/api/carrier/register` + compliance@ flag on every duplicate hit + enumeration hygiene.** NO SMS path. NO OTP verification. NO A2P dependency per directive — duplicate-detection + anti-abuse only. Closes the phone/email asymmetry: pre-ala `registerCarrier` rejected duplicate emails (`User.email` unique constraint + `findUnique` at [carrierController.ts:27-30](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts#L27) → 409 "Email already registered") but had no phone duplicate check at all. Same endpoint had **zero rate limiter** — wide open to enumeration scraping. No compliance signal on collision today. **Phase A audit confirmed:** (a) `express-rate-limit ^7.5.0` already in backend/package.json — no new dep; (b) phone stored as-typed on `User.phone` (e.g. `"(269) 220-6760"`); two local 10-digit strip helpers exist in `chameleonDetectionService` + `crossReferenceService` for fingerprint hashing but neither feeds duplicate-checks; no E.164 normalizer anywhere; (c) registration is US-only — no country picker, frontend assumes +1; (d) compliance flag pattern: two shapes coexist (chameleon → role-based dispatch via `User WHERE role IN ['ADMIN','OPERATIONS']`; insuranceVerify → `COMPLIANCE_EMAIL` constant from `backend/src/config/authority.ts:86`). Directive specifies "flag to compliance@" — using `COMPLIANCE_EMAIL` constant pattern. **Five files touched:** (1) NEW [`backend/src/lib/phoneNormalization.ts`](backend/src/lib/phoneNormalization.ts) — `normalizePhoneE164(raw): string | null` + `phoneNumbersMatch(a, b): boolean` helpers (~60 LOC w/ docs). Strips non-digits + handles leading `1` country code + prepends `+1` to 10-digit US national number; returns null on any input that doesn't normalize to exactly 10 US digits. Header comment documents the libphonenumber-js swap target when/if international registration ships — every caller inherits the upgrade transparently. (2) [`backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts`](backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts) — phone duplicate check inserted between email check and DOT/MC checks at ~line 125. Both stored + submitted numbers normalized to E.164 before comparison (light scan at pre-revenue volumes; the indexed `phoneNormalized` column refactor is banked at ~10K+ rows as a separate sprint). Also new `flagRegistrationDuplicate(opts)` helper (~80 LOC) — fires brief alert to `COMPLIANCE_EMAIL` + writes `SystemLog WARNING` row with `logType=SECURITY` + `source="registration-duplicate"`. Both wrapped in `.catch()` so neither blocks the 409 response. Email body + SystemLog details carry **SHA-256 hash (first 16 chars)** of the colliding value — never plaintext PII (defense against accidental log-table PII exposure + against compliance@-inbox-compromise scenario surfacing the attempted values). `registrationIp` + `registrationCountry` capture moved earlier in the function so the compliance flag has forensic context (pre-ala these were captured after duplicate checks; duplicate hits had no IP/country signal because the variables didn't exist yet). Email + phone collision branches both fire the flag before returning. (3) [`backend/src/routes/carrier.ts`](backend/src/routes/carrier.ts) — two new rate limiters mounted on `POST /register`: (a) `registerIpLimiter` — **10 attempts / 15 min** keyed by `req.ip`, fires BEFORE multer (no body needed); (b) `registerContactLimiter` — **3 attempts / 60 min** keyed by composite `${lowercaseEmail}|${digitOnlyPhone}`, fires AFTER multer + body normalization middleware so `req.body.email` + `req.body.phone` are populated. Composite-key fallback to `ip:${req.ip}` if both email + phone absent (malformed request) — never returns empty string which express-rate-limit would treat as a global counter. Skip-handler returns 429 "Too many registration attempts. Please try again later." — generic per enumeration hygiene (no echo of which limiter fired or against which key). (4) [`frontend/src/components/ui/VersionFooter.tsx`](frontend/src/components/ui/VersionFooter.tsx) — version bump + comment block. (5) CLAUDE.md §11 row (this row). **Enumeration hygiene (directive §4):** duplicate error responses carry NO name / MC / agency echo. Email collide → 409 `"Email already registered"` (unchanged from today). Phone collide → 409 `"Phone number already registered"` (mirrors). Rate-limit hit → 429 with generic message. Compliance flag dispatch happens AFTER the 409 response is sent, so duplicate-hit response time is indistinguishable from non-collision response from the attacker's clock — closes the timing-side-channel that would otherwise leak whether email/phone existed before the response fully landed. **Architectural pattern banked (consistent with v3.8.akz unified-gate convention):** when a side-effect (compliance flag dispatch) shares a common trigger surface (any duplicate hit on registration), the side-effect logic lives as a single helper function alongside the surface. Controllers pass authoritative context (collidedField + attemptedValue + attemptIp + attemptCountry); the helper handles dispatch + logging + PII-hashing uniformly. Future sprints adding a third collision class (e.g. DOT/MC duplicate) inherit the same helper signature. **Banked for future sprints (out of v3.8.ala scope):** (a) Email lowercase-on-lookup to close the case-sensitivity enumeration vector — `Test@example.com` ≠ `test@example.com` today on Postgres + Prisma `findUnique`. Pre-existing gap surfaced by Phase A audit. (b) `User.phone @unique` schema constraint + backfill — race window stays today since the duplicate check is application-level only. Pre-revenue volumes make this acceptable; race window closes via DB constraint when N grows. (c) libphonenumber-js adoption — only when international registration ships. Current DIY normalizer is sufficient for US-only scope. (d) Indexed `phoneNormalized` column on `User` — refactor when N > ~10K carriers. Today's full scan over phone-bearing users is light at pre-revenue volumes. (e) **OTP verification path / SMS verification — explicitly held for next sprint** pending Wasi's SMS-path decision per directive. (f) Backend required-doc validation gate — pre-existing gap from v3.8.aky banking; continues to defer. **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend `tsc --noEmit` clean, frontend `tsc --noEmit` + `npx next build` clean. **No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new package install** — `express-rate-limit` already in deps per Phase A. Per §3.1: v3.8.akz → v3.8.ala sequence-continuous. **Letter:** akz latest origin/main HEAD; ala sequence-continuous on top per the §3.1 double-letter continuation (after `akz` comes `ala`, NOT `alz` or any other variant). |

| v3.8.akz | 2026-05-25 | **Insurance-agent verification email — wired into registerCarrier + unified gate across all callsites (Item 1 Path β).** Pre-akz forensic state per the v3.8.aky Phase A audit: the email sender at [`backend/src/services/insuranceVerificationService.ts:83-243`](backend/src/services/insuranceVerificationService.ts#L83) was healthy + correctly recipient-routed to `carrier.insuranceAgentEmail`, but `registerCarrier` never invoked it — the original "all 4 agent fields populated → send to agent" design intent never landed on the registration path. Only three callsites existed: (a) `updateCarrier` admin endpoint at `carrierController.ts:1185`, (b) the `PATCH /carrier-compliance` insurance update route at `carrierCompliance.ts:286`, and (c) the expiry-reminder cron at `insuranceVerificationService.ts:285`. Existing gates were also drift-degraded — single-field check (`updated.insuranceAgentEmail` only) rather than the "all 4 agent fields populated" intent. Net pre-akz behavior: carrier completes onboarding with all 4 agent fields populated → agent receives nothing; agent only learns SRL is verifying their carrier client if/when AE updates the record post-registration. **Path β implementation (unified gate via new helper):** new exported `maybeSendInsuranceVerificationEmail(carrierId, insuranceFieldsChanged: boolean)` in insuranceVerificationService.ts with two-condition gate — (a) CHANGE-CONDITION (`insuranceFieldsChanged` boolean passed by caller; caller knows whether their write touched any insurance-relevant field; prevents re-sends on unrelated PATCHes like status flip, address-only update, scorecard refresh); (b) COMPLETENESS-CONDITION (all 4 agent fields — name, email, phone, agency — populated on the RESULTING persisted record; reads `prisma.carrierProfile.findUnique` AFTER the caller's write completed, NOT the request payload, so a PATCH touching one agent field still evaluates correctly against the union of prior + new state). Returns `{ sent: boolean, reason?: string }` so callsites log skip-reasons at info level for AE forensic visibility. Also exported `didInsuranceFieldsChange(data)` — inventory of 12 insurance-relevant fields (4 coverage provider/amount pairs + 4 agent fields), returns true if any are present in the write payload. Used by `updateCarrier` + `registerCarrier` to compute the change-condition uniformly. **Three callsite changes:** (1) `registerCarrier` at carrierController.ts:387 (new Step 2.5 in the post-response fire-and-forget chain, between admin-notify Step 2 and chameleon-fingerprint Step 3) — NEW call. Change-condition computed via `didInsuranceFieldsChange(req.body)`; completeness enforced inside the helper. (2) `updateCarrier` at carrierController.ts:1185 — replaces the prior 7-field hard-coded `insuranceFieldsUpdated` array + single-field gate. Now `didInsuranceFieldsChange(data) + maybeSendInsuranceVerificationEmail(updated.id, ...)`. (3) `carrierCompliance.ts:286` — replaces the prior `if (updated.insuranceAgentEmail)` single-field check. Change-condition trivially `true` (this route's PATCH semantic is "carrier updated their insurance record" by routing definition); completeness gate still applies via the helper's post-write DB read. **Cron path (`checkExpiringInsurance`) UNCHANGED** — different semantic (periodic reminder, not a "fields changed" event); cron's existing `daysUntil === 60 || 30 || 7` gate is correct for the reminder loop. Routing the cron through the unified gate would incorrectly skip reminders whenever insurance fields hadn't changed in the most recent write — the whole point of the cron is to remind on time-thresholds regardless of recent writes. **Architectural pattern banked (consistent with v3.8.akw notifyTenderAction fan-out at all five accept surfaces):** when multiple callsites share a common gate + side effect, the gate logic lives in the service layer alongside the side effect. Controllers + route handlers pass authoritative inputs (post-write record reads + change-condition booleans) — they don't reimplement gate logic. Future sprints adding a 5th callsite (e.g. AE bulk insurance import, third-party COI sync webhook) inherit the gate automatically. **Files: 4 (insuranceVerificationService.ts +60/0 LOC unified gate helper + INSURANCE_RELEVANT_FIELDS constant + didInsuranceFieldsChange helper, carrierController.ts +30/-12 LOC unified-gate wiring at updateCarrier + NEW registerCarrier Step 2.5 call, carrierCompliance.ts +12/-5 LOC unified-gate wiring at PATCH /insurance endpoint, VersionFooter.tsx footer block + version bump, CLAUDE.md §11 row).** Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11: backend tsc --noEmit clean, frontend tsc --noEmit + npx next build clean (no frontend source changes; gates run defensively). No Prisma migration. No new env vars. No new dependencies. Per §3.1: v3.8.aky → v3.8.akz sequence-continuous. **§13.3 Item closure:** the "insurance-agent verification flow NOT FIRING TODAY" finding from v3.8.aky Phase A is now LIVE on the registration path. Future polish: AE-facing UI to view sent verification emails per carrier (banked but out of akz scope), agent-side reply tracking (out of scope — no inbound webhook today). **Letter:** aky latest origin/main HEAD; akz sequence-continuous on top. |

| v3.8.aky | 2026-05-25 | **Carrier onboarding hardening — browser-autofill opt-out on First Name + Last Name (Item 2) + Workers' Compensation Coverage 4th required document card (Item 3) + risk-flagging cron disable carry-forward from prior turn.** **Item 2 (Sub-pattern 14 fire #30 — diff-disproves-hypothesis halt):** live repro reported as "FMCSA writes city ('Skokie') into First Name and state ('IL') into Last Name". Sub-rule c authoritative-source verification on `applyFmcsaData` at [`frontend/src/app/onboarding/page.tsx:291-302`](frontend/src/app/onboarding/page.tsx#L291) confirmed zero mapping writes to firstName/lastName — FMCSA QCMobile census endpoint doesn't return person-name fields at all (verified backend `fmcsaService.ts` `FMCSACarrierResult` interface + `routes/carrier.ts:29-95` response shape). Hypothesis was disproven by diff before any fix shipped. Actual cause: Chrome's saved-address-card heuristic was injecting saved-profile city/state into the now-unprotected `firstName`/`lastName` inputs at lines 979/983 — those inputs carried default `name="firstName"`/`"lastName"` matching Chrome's autofill match keys, and v3.8.aiu had only applied the autofill opt-out to email/phone/password (not the two name inputs). **Fix:** apply the v3.8.aiu pattern verbatim — `autoComplete="off"` + non-standard `name="carrier-registration-firstname"` / `"-lastname"` on both inputs (~4 LOC + ~10 LOC comment block). NO pattern validator added per directive — `pattern="^[a-zA-Z\s'-]..."` would red-border legitimate accented and non-Latin names (José, Nguyễn, and similar) common across the owner-operator base; the opt-out alone closes the observed repro. **Item 3 (Sub-pattern 6 sub-rule c fire #31 — false-enum directive caught pre-edit):** locked directive specified "add the dedicated DocType enum value WORKERS_COMP with its migration — do not bury it under OTHER with filename tagging". Phase B opening read of [`schema.prisma:2009`](backend/prisma/schema.prisma#L2009) confirmed `docType String?` is NOT a Prisma enum — it's a free-form String column with the accepted values documented as an inline comment. `grep "^enum DocType"` against schema returns zero matches. The audit-explore agent's reference to "the `docType` enum's available values" was loose wording — there is no `enum DocType { ... }` block to add a value to. Halted Phase B, surfaced 3 paths to user (γ — string-canonical no migration / δ — full enum conversion / hold Item 3). User ratified **Path γ — string-canonical, no migration**. Achieves the directive's stated intent (clean `WHERE docType = 'WORKERS_COMP'` queryability, not buried under `OTHER` with filename disambiguation) without any schema migration; the Item 8.10 Neon-direct verification dependency dissolves with no migration to verify. Two sub-rule c fires within a single sprint Phase B is unusual — the methodology library is doing real work on this sprint. Cumulative §19 registry advances 29 → 31. **Frontend changes (`frontend/src/app/onboarding/page.tsx`):** (a) new 4th docs-array entry at lines 1386-1391 — `{ key: "wc", label: "Workers' Compensation Coverage", desc: "Certificate of WC insurance OR signed exemption affidavit (single-driver operators with no employees may submit exemption per state law)", required: true }`. Accepts either format per the operational reality that single-driver authorities with no employees are WC-exempt in many states; the affidavit IS acceptable proof. (b) `canNext` step===2 gate at lines 399-409 extended with `if (!hasDoc("wc")) return false;` line. (c) Required-count banner array at line 1370 extended to `["w9", "insurance", "authority", "wc", ...(hasCanadianOps ? ["safety"] : [])]` — banner reads 4 of 4 (or 5 of 5 with Canadian ops + Safety Cert). (d) Pre-existing COI desc tightened — was "Auto liability, cargo, general liability, **and workers' comp**" → "Auto liability, cargo, and general liability coverage". COI desc no longer implies WC is bundled into the COI now that WC has its own card. **Backend changes (`backend/src/controllers/carrierController.ts:175-185`):** `DOC_TYPE_MAP` gains `wc: { docType: "WORKERS_COMP", flagField: null }` entry. | v3.8.ajb | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Sprint F (required-documents enforcement) + critical EIN registration regression hotfix (atomic bundle).** Two related Step 3 / submit-path fixes shipped together since both touch `onboarding/page.tsx` + both block carrier onboarding. **Critical bug fix (regression from v3.8.air):** reproduced via curl against deployed prod — `POST /api/carrier/register` with `ein:""` → 400 `{"error":"Validation failed","details":[{"field":"ein","message":"EIN must be 9 digits"}]}`. In v3.8.air I removed the EIN input from Step 1 but kept `ein: ""` in form state with the comment *"retained as empty default for backend payload compatibility — backend will gracefully accept empty string"*. That assumption was wrong. Backend Zod is `ein: z.string().regex(/^\d{9}$/).optional()` — `.optional()` accepts ABSENT, but PRESENT-with-empty-string fails the regex (zero digits ≠ nine). `regData` spread in `handleSubmit` included `ein: ""`, so every carrier registration since v3.8.air had been silently failing at Step 5 Submit. Wasi caught it on the first real end-to-end registration through deployed v3.8.aja. **Fix:** 3-line frontend change — peel `ein` out of regData destructure via `ein: einFromForm`; conditionally include only if non-empty: `...(einFromForm ? { ein: einFromForm } : {})`. Backend `.optional()` accepts absent field cleanly. **Methodology lesson:** smoke verification on form sprints must include FULL submit path, not just step-level visual checks. `feedback_visual_smoke_before_push.md` covers layout/chrome but doesn't enumerate end-to-end submit verification. Banking as extension: for any sprint touching `handleSubmit` payload shape OR backend Zod schema, smoke MUST include a real curl-or-browser submit through to 200/400 + response body inspection. **Sprint F (required documents enforcement):** Step 3 documents (W-9, Insurance Certificate, Authority Letter + Safety Fitness Certificate if Canadian) were UI-visible but completely optional at the canNext gate — carriers could proceed without uploading any. (1) `canNext` extended for `step === 2` with per-key `hasDoc()` checks; Safety Cert conditional on `hasCanadianOps`. (2) Document data extended with `required: true` flag on all visible docs (Safety Cert already conditional per v3.8.aiv). (3) Visual indicators: red asterisk `text-[#9B2C2C]` after required doc labels; missing-state warning-amber bg+border (`bg-[#FBEFD4] border-[#B07A1A]/40`); description text appends " — required" when missing; upload icon switches to amber. (4) Count banner above doc list "X of Y required uploaded" — green when all met, amber otherwise. (5) Section intro extends with "All marked with * are required to submit." **Scope:** ~50 LOC in onboarding/page.tsx (3 LOC EIN + ~45 LOC required-docs UI + canNext block). No backend changes, no CSP changes, no new dependencies. **Pre-commit gates (Sub-pattern 11 CI parity):** frontend tsc --noEmit clean; frontend npx next build clean (`/onboarding` 17.2 kB → 17.5 kB). **Letter:** aja latest origin/main HEAD; ajb sequence-continuous on top. Cloudflare deploy only — no backend changes, Render path-filter skips this push (per aiy retry-mechanic learning, which is fine). **Post-deploy verification:** (1) EIN hotfix — register a test carrier through full flow to Step 5 + Submit, should now succeed (no more "Validation failed"). (2) Required docs — try Next without uploading any, button stays disabled; upload W-9 + Insurance + Authority → enables (no Canadian region) or also require Safety Cert (Canadian region selected). (3) Count banner updates live as each doc uploads. **Patterns applied:** §3.5 audit-first (curl-reproduced the EIN regression before fixing); §3.3 atomic single-file ship with bundled fixes; §3.4 halt > ship (paused Sprint F push to diagnose EIN first instead of pushing on top); §3.2 visual smoke; §19 Sub-pattern 11 CI-parity. **Patterns emerged:** methodology lesson — **submit-path-smoke as required gate** for any sprint touching handleSubmit / Zod schema. Visual chrome can pass eye-test perfectly while a payload-shape regression sits in the hot path for many sprints. Extension of `feedback_visual_smoke_before_push` banked. |

| v3.8.ajv | 2026-05-24 (this commit) | **Security hotfix bundle (C1 + C2 + C6) from blindsides audit P0.** Three critical security bugs caught during the post-Phase-1-7 blindsides re-audit, all clustering in the rate-confirmation + password-change surfaces. Atomic ship — small, no migration, validates the post-v3.8.ajs clean deploy chain. **C1 — RC PDF download authorization bypass (CRITICAL — financial espionage vector):** `downloadRateConfirmationPdf` controller at `rateConfirmationController.ts:163` was authorized to the CARRIER role per `routes/rateConfirmations.ts:24` but had no per-record check that the logged-in CARRIER actually owns the load. Any logged-in carrier could enumerate RC IDs (cuid format) and download any other carrier's full RC PDF — including carrier cost, fuel surcharge, accessorials, and margin. Direct competitive intelligence harvest path. **Fix:** added `if (req.user.role === "CARRIER" && rc.load.carrierId !== req.user.id) return 403` after the findUnique. AE-side roles (BROKER/ADMIN/CEO/DISPATCH/OPERATIONS/ACCOUNTING) bypass — they need to download any carrier's RC during normal review workflow. **C2 — RC sign authorization bypass (signature forgery vector):** `signRateConfirmation` controller at line 197 had the same surface — endpoint authorized to CARRIER per route line 25, no per-record ownership check. Any logged-in carrier could sign any other carrier's RC, falsifying commitment + corrupting audit trail (the `carrierSignature` field would store the wrong signer's name). **Fix:** same role-conditional gate pattern as C1. Added `load: { select: { carrierId: true } }` to the existing `findUnique` to make the carrierId available. AE roles still pass through for the "mark as signed on behalf" operational case (carrier emailed a wet signature outside the portal). **C6 — Password downgrade vector:** registration validator at `validators/carrier.ts:10-15` enforces γ "Very Strong" tier (14+ chars + uppercase + lowercase + digit + special). Pre-ajv the `changePasswordSchema` + `forceChangePasswordSchema` at `carrierAuth.ts:46-52` accepted `min(8)` only. Account-takeover path: attacker who briefly gets in via credential stuffing downgrades to `12345678`, retains access after legitimate carrier resets to a new strong password. **Fix:** new shared `STRONG_PASSWORD` Zod chain mirroring the registration's exact 5-rule validation. Applied to both `changePasswordSchema.newPassword` and `forceChangePasswordSchema.newPassword`. HIBP not re-checked server-side per the registration's same precedent (frontend handles HIBP via k-anonymity API; backend enforces composition as defense-in-depth). **Scope:** ~50 LOC net across 2 backend files (rateConfirmationController.ts + carrierAuth.ts). No frontend changes. No migration. **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **P1002 risk:** zero — no migrate deploy needed. Validates the post-v3.8.ajs clean deploy chain is fully operational. **Other P0 items remaining banked (blindsides audit):** v3.8.ajw — state-machine integrity bundle (central validateLoadStatusTransition + notification cleanup cron + audit log on declineTender/counterTender + RC failure recovery from acceptTender's fire-and-forget); v3.8.ajx — operational compliance bundle (insurance auto-suspend active-load gate + SUSPENDED gates on /:id/status + /:id/documents + /:id/check-call + QP monthly limits + Compass vetting failure telemetry + OTP override admin tool). **Letter:** aju → ajv sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.aju | 2026-05-24 | **Shipper tracking URL display fix — B4 audit framing correction.** Phase 5 audit agent flagged "Public `/track/[token]` route is missing entirely" as a P0 bug. Re-audit during ajt implementation revealed the actual infrastructure: **(a)** `/track.html` static page exists at `frontend/public/track.html` (793 LOC, vanilla JS reads `window.location.pathname` + fetches `/api/tracking/:token`); **(b)** `frontend/public/_redirects:41` has the SPA-rewrite rule `/track/* /track.html 200` that serves the static page for any `/track/<anything>` URL while preserving the URL in the address bar for bookmarking; **(c)** `shipperLoadNotifyService.ts:48` + `:316` generate the canonical email/QR URL pattern `${PORTAL_BASE}/track/${load.trackingToken}` which matches the rewrite rule. The full chain works end-to-end. The actual bug was a 1-line display-copy mismatch at `frontend/src/app/dashboard/track-trace/tabs/TrackingTab.tsx:122` showing AE the wrong URL pattern: `track.silkroutelogistics.ai/s/{token}` — wrong subdomain (`track.*` doesn't exist; canonical is apex) AND wrong path (`/s/` doesn't match the rewrite rule). AE would see this URL and either (a) try to share it with shipper → 404 on a non-existent subdomain, or (b) construct manually → mismatch with the actual URL printed on BOL QR + emailed in tracking-link notifications. Fix: corrected to `silkroutelogistics.ai/track/{token}` matching the canonical pattern. Inline code-comment explains the chain so any future agent re-audit doesn't repeat the "route missing" misinterpretation. **Methodology lesson banked (audit-agent verification gate):** the Phase 5 agent's "no `/track/[token]/page.tsx` route exists" framing was technically accurate (no Next.js dynamic route file) but missed the canonical solution shipped at v3.8.aae Sprint 26 — the SPA-rewrite-via-_redirects pattern documented in CLAUDE.md §11. Audit agents reading code-side without cross-referencing `_redirects` + `public/*.html` static surfaces can produce false-positive "missing" findings. Sub-rule c sub-pattern fire candidate: *"When an agent classifies a feature as missing, verify the canonical SHIPPED solution architecture (not just the assumed-shape solution architecture)."* Awaits fire #2 + #3 from independent incidents for canonical promotion. **Scope:** 1-line code change + ~12-line inline comment + this docs row. **Pre-commit gates:** frontend tsc + next build clean. **No backend changes. No migration.** **Letter:** ajt → aju sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajt | 2026-05-24 | **Carrier portal critical-bug bundle (B1+B2+B3+B5) from Phase 1-7 audit.** Four bugs caught during the post-ajr 7-phase carrier-portal audit, all shipping as one atomic commit since they cluster around the carrier-facing post-approval lifecycle. **B1 (Phase 4 — RC visibility):** `Load.rateConfirmationPdfUrl` schema field has existed since pre-ajt but was NEVER populated by any write path. Result: carrier-portal RC download link at `/carrier/dashboard/my-loads` line 183 was gated on the field and always invisible despite the `GET /api/rate-confirmations/:id/pdf` endpoint being CARRIER-authorized + functional. Fix: in `sendRateConfirmation` controller (line 140-148), after RC status flip to SENT, also `prisma.load.update` with `rateConfirmationPdfUrl: /api/rate-confirmations/${rc.id}/pdf`. Carrier portal link now resolves to the existing endpoint; httpOnly cookie auths it on click. Closes §13.3 Item 170. **B2 (Phase 6 — invoice automation):** `autoGenerateInvoice(loadId)` service exists in `invoiceService.ts:5` but was never called from the POD upload endpoint at `carrierLoads.ts:413`. Result: carrier delivered + uploaded POD + no invoice ever generated automatically. Fix: trigger `autoGenerateInvoice(load.id)` from the POD upload block when the new POD-advancing status flip lands on POD_RECEIVED. Fire-and-forget non-blocking pattern; service is idempotent (checks for existing invoice on loadId). **B3 (Phase 6 — POD status flip):** audit agent's "inverted logic" framing was technically incorrect (the ternary at line 467 read correctly), but the underlying UX gap was real — most carriers upload POD at `AT_DELIVERY` (the POD IS their proof of completing delivery) before manually marking DELIVERED in the portal. Pre-ajt only DELIVERED → POD_RECEIVED was wired, so loads stayed at AT_DELIVERY with POD attached. Fix: extend the advancing-statuses list to `["AT_DELIVERY", "DELIVERED", "LOADED"]` → `"POD_RECEIVED"`. Earlier statuses (BOOKED/DISPATCHED/AT_PICKUP/IN_TRANSIT) still don't auto-advance — uploading POD when you haven't reached the destination is likely an error. **B5 (Phase 1 — approval email gap):** AE-side approve action pre-ajt went through `POST /carrier/verify/${id}` with `{ status: "APPROVED" }` which (a) didn't fire a carrier-facing approval email, (b) didn't write a dedicated `AuditAction.APPROVE` row (used generic UPDATE), (c) had no notification fan-out. Result: carriers learned they were approved only by happening to log in and seeing the dashboard switch from status page to main dashboard. Fix: new `backend/src/services/approvalService.ts` with `approveCarrier({ carrierId, approvedById, note })` — atomic transaction flips `onboardingStatus → APPROVED` + sets `approvedAt` + clears any prior rejection metadata defensively + syncs legacy `User.isVerified` (kept in lockstep with 27+ existing readers per CLAUDE.md §11 v3.8.aje note). Service validates not-already-APPROVED + not-SUSPENDED. Fires brand-canonical carrier email "Welcome to the Caravan Partner Program" (reply-to operations@) with optional AE note + portal CTA + green success palette + creates in-app Notification row (type ONBOARDING) for the bell. New endpoint `POST /api/carriers/:id/approve` (ADMIN/CEO + Zod + `auditLog("APPROVE", "Carrier")`). AE Console mutation routed to this endpoint when status === "APPROVED"; legacy `/carrier/verify/${id}` path retained as fallback. **Note on `approvedById`:** CarrierProfile has only `approvedAt` (no `approvedById` column); audit trail captures the AE via the AuditLog row. Schema migration to add `approvedById` banked as small follow-up if AE forensics needs per-row attribution. **Scope:** ~210 LOC net across 4 files (new approvalService.ts + carriers.ts route + carrierLoads.ts POD block + rateConfirmationController.ts send block + carriers/page.tsx mutation route swap). **Pre-commit gates per Sub-pattern 11:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **No migration.** **P1002 risk:** zero — no migrate deploy needed. **Banked for v3.8.aju (next commit):** B4 — public `/track/[token]` Next.js dynamic route for the shipper tracking link. TrackingTab references the URL but no route exists. Separate atomic ship per §3.3. **Other audit findings banked for future sprints (v3.8.ajv+):** decline reason persistence (Item 90), counter-tender email (Item 89), central state machine validator (Item 159), ACH banking self-service, dispute filing UI, CarrierFraudBanner in portal, profile-completeness banner, Marco Polo carrier context, notification inbox UX, RC PDF polish (transit hours, T&C body, versioning, email tracking binding), photo capture at AT_PICKUP/LOADED, GPS auto-capture, carrier in-app check-call reminders, shipper pickup email auto-trigger on status flip, ELD device mapping UI, email-verify reminder cron, auto-approve messaging on success screen, Compass vetting failure telemetry, status field vs onboardingStatus drift cleanup. **Letter:** ajs → ajt sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajs | 2026-05-24 | **Defensive build-time DIRECT_URL guard + diagnostic for v3.8.ajr P1002 recurrence.** Render deploy of v3.8.ajr hit P1002 again despite the v3.8.ajg DIRECT_URL split being in place. Build log smoking gun: `Datasource "db": ... at "ep-green-frog-ajsgv9me-pooler.c-3.us-east-2.aws.neon.tech"` — Prisma migrate was STILL routing through the pooler (note `-pooler` in hostname). Root cause: the `DIRECT_URL` env var on Render WAS set (Prisma generate succeeded — would have P1012'd otherwise), but its **value contained the pooled URL by mistake**. Wasi presumably copied from Neon's "Connect" modal without toggling pooling off → both DATABASE_URL and DIRECT_URL pointed at the same pooled endpoint → DIRECT_URL split was decorative, not functional. **Permanent fix shipped:** new `backend/scripts/check-direct-url.js` pre-migrate guard added to canonical buildCommand at §2.2 (between `npm run build` and `npx prisma migrate deploy`). Fails fast (exit 1) with self-explanatory remediation message if (a) `DIRECT_URL` is unset OR (b) `DIRECT_URL` hostname contains `-pooler`. Also surfaces a non-fatal warning if `DATABASE_URL` and `DIRECT_URL` point at different Neon projects (sanity check against typos). Pre-ajs the failure mode was a silent 10s P1002 timeout with no hint about WHY; post-ajs the build fails in <1s with the exact Render-dashboard remediation steps + Neon-dashboard "toggle pooling off" guidance + four-location checklist reference. **Tested with 3 scenarios:** (1) DIRECT_URL unset → fails with "env var is not set" + remediation; (2) DIRECT_URL with pooler hostname → fails with "hostname contains '-pooler'" + cause explanation; (3) DIRECT_URL correctly direct → exits 0 with `✓ DIRECT_URL configured correctly`. URL parsing via `new URL()` constructor (handles edge cases like passwords with `@` characters). Sanity-check: parses DATABASE_URL similarly + warns (not fails) if the two URLs point at different Neon projects (e.g., one's hostname starts with `ep-foo` and the other with `ep-bar` — that's almost certainly a typo). **Scope:** ~110 LOC (new script) + 2-line buildCommand updates in CLAUDE.md §2.2 + render.yaml. **Banks methodology principle:** the four-location checklist for env() schema reference additions from §19 Sub-pattern 11 case study #2 (CLAUDE.md §19 ci-hotfix retrospective) is now enforced as CODE, not just docs. **§19 Sub-pattern 11 fire #3 banked** — third surface where local pre-commit gates passed but Render deploy failed due to env-config mismatch (Sprint 51 #1: JSX ESLint; v3.8.ajg → ci-hotfix #2: CI workflow env block; v3.8.ajr → ajs #3: Render env var value-not-just-presence). Three-fire validation lineage now satisfied per §19 sub-rule c three-fire canonical promotion convention. Fire count for next quarterly §19 review: 26 → 27. **POST-DEPLOY required (Wasi):** (1) Fix the existing `DIRECT_URL` env var value on Render dashboard to point at Neon's DIRECT (unpooled) endpoint. (2) Update Render dashboard buildCommand to include the new `node scripts/check-direct-url.js` step between `npm run build` and `npx prisma migrate deploy`. (3) Manual Deploy to apply the v3.8.ajh→ajr pending migrations + verify the guard runs + verify migrate routes through direct endpoint (build log should now show hostname WITHOUT `-pooler`). **Letter:** ajr → ajs sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajr | 2026-05-24 | **Step 3 doc-upload silent-fail fix (W-9 / COI / Authority / Safety now actually reach S3 + carrier documents tab).** Wasi confirmed during smoke test of v3.8.ajq: "When I attempted the test carrier and uploaded the test docs they didn't appear under the pending carrier profile documents tab." Pre-ajr the Step 3 file picker (added v3.8.aiv) staged files with a `__docType` property tag (`w9` / `insurance` / `authority` / `safety`) into `files: File[]` state; `handleSubmit` then attempted a separate `POST /api/carrier/documents` with `Authorization: Bearer ${data.token}` — but the v3.8.ajd login refactor stopped issuing JWTs at registration, so `data.token` was undefined and every doc upload silently 401'd. Required documents enforcement (v3.8.ajb Sprint F) gate-checked at the canNext layer but never verified that the files actually persisted. Net effect: every carrier since v3.8.ajd selected required docs in Step 3, hit Submit, saw success, and their documents vanished to /dev/null. Fixed in v3.8.ajq (which removed the broken upload block) only stopped the silent failure; ajr restores the actual upload path. **Backend changes:** (1) `routes/carrier.ts` `/register` route — `upload.fields([...])` extended with `{ name: "files", maxCount: 10 }` for the Step 3 staged docs. Normalization middleware extended with (a) `docTypes` array normalization mirroring the existing `equipmentTypes` / `operatingRegions` pattern, (b) numeric string → number coercion for 5 fields (`numberOfTrucks`, `autoLiabilityAmount`, `cargoInsuranceAmount`, `generalLiabilityAmount`, `workersCompAmount`) — required because FormData fields are always strings while Zod schema declares `z.number()`, (c) boolean string → boolean coercion for 3 fields (`additionalInsuredSRL`, `waiverOfSubrogation`, `thirtyDayCancellationNotice`). Empty-string scrub for both classes so optional fields pass `z.optional()` cleanly. (2) `carrierController.ts` `registerCarrier` — new block after the existing `photoId` + `articlesOfInc` upload paths. Iterates `req.files.files` paired by index with `req.body.docTypes[]`; `DOC_TYPE_MAP` translates frontend tag (`w9` / `insurance` / `authority` / `safety`) into canonical Document.docType (`W9` / `COI` / `AUTHORITY` / `OTHER` — `SAFETY_CERT` isn't in the Document.docType enum comment at schema.prisma:1945, classified `OTHER` with the type evident in the storage path + filename) and the CarrierProfile boolean flag to flip (`w9Uploaded` / `insuranceCertUploaded` / `authorityDocUploaded`; safety has no flag field). Files land in S3 at `carrier-docs/{profileId}/{tagKey}-{ts}-{rand}{ext}`; Document rows tagged `entityType="CARRIER"`, `entityId=profileId`, `uploadSource="CARRIER_PORTAL"`, `status="PENDING"`. **Frontend changes (`onboarding/page.tsx`):** `handleSubmit` switched from `JSON.stringify` body + `Content-Type: application/json` header to a FormData object. Scalar fields appended as strings (booleans → `"true"` / `"false"`); arrays as repeated keys; files appended as `files` + parallel `docTypes` form-field array (paired by index). Browser auto-sets `Content-Type: multipart/form-data; boundary=...` when body is a FormData instance — explicit Content-Type header REMOVED (would break multer's boundary parsing). **Compatibility note:** backend route was already configured with `upload.fields()` since pre-ajr (photoId + articlesOfInc fields), but the FRONTEND was sending JSON, so multer acted as passthrough (multer only engages on multipart Content-Type). The photoId + articlesOfInc fields were aspirational — frontend never sent them. Now they continue to be ignored, only `files` (Step 3 staged docs) come through. **Scope:** ~110 LOC across 3 files (route normalization middleware + controller doc-upload block + frontend FormData rewrite). **Pre-commit gates:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **Post-deploy verification:** register test carrier via /onboarding incognito → upload 3 required docs in Step 3 (W-9 / COI / Authority) → submit → success screen → log in as ADMIN → open the new carrier in /dashboard/carriers → Documents tab should show 3 entries with correct docType labels (W9 / COI / AUTHORITY). Document Completeness section on Compliance tab should show 3 booleans flipped. **P1002 risk:** zero — no migration. **Letter:** ajq → ajr sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajq | 2026-05-24 | **Post-register redirect bug fix + application reference number + email-verify CTA.** Wasi caught during deployed smoke test on v3.8.ajp arc: after submitting a real carrier application via `/onboarding`, the page silently redirected to `/dashboard/overview` (the AE Console root) instead of showing the success screen. The success screen with summary card + "What Happens Next" had existed since v3.8.aiz Sprint D but was unreachable on a real successful submit — the `setSuccess(true)` only fired in the catch block on NETWORK failure (lines 498-499 of the pre-ajq onboarding/page.tsx), and the happy path hit `router.push("/dashboard/overview")` at line 495. Wasi observed the symptom as "logged me direct into my user portal" because he was already logged in as ADMIN during testing and the dashboard route succeeded; an unauthenticated carrier would have been bounced to /auth/login by the dashboard layout's auth check — also wrong, just different. **Backend fix (`carrierController.ts`):** added `applicationReference = APP-{user.id.slice(-8).toUpperCase()}` to the 201 response. Derived from user.id last 8 chars uppercased (cuid format → mostly alphanumeric, stable, no schema change). Format example: `APP-A1B2C3D4`. Carrier saves it for support correspondence; AE looks carriers up via email/MC/DOT search on `/dashboard/carriers` so the reference is purely a carrier-facing handle. **Frontend fix (`onboarding/page.tsx`):** (1) new `applicationReference` state captures the value from the API response; (2) replaced `router.push("/dashboard/overview")` with `setSuccess(true)` on the happy path — the success screen now renders on actual successful registration, not just on network failure; (3) success screen Header card extended with prominent reference number display (mono font, gold-dark eyebrow + navy value, cream surface); (4) NEW "Next Steps to Begin Your Caravan Journey" card added between Header and Application Summary — two-step CTA: (a) verify your email (with explicit subject line `"Confirm your email — Silk Route Logistics"` so carrier knows what to look for in inbox), (b) log in to track via `/carrier/login` (NOT `/auth/login` which is AE — non-APPROVED carriers route through carrier portal per v3.8.ajd login refactor). Gold-dark CTA button styling. **Removed code:** legacy doc-upload block at lines 486-494 (used `Bearer ${data.token}` but no JWT was issued at registration post-v3.8.ajd → silent 401 → Step 3 staged files never reached S3). Banked as **v3.8.ajr** for proper fix — accept multipart on `/api/carrier/register` OR upload attachments via the verification-link flow. Step 3 file picker still works as a UI affordance (carriers can select files) but submission no longer attempts the broken auth'd upload. **Stale comment cleared:** "Token is set as httpOnly cookie by backend — no localStorage needed" at line 484 was wrong post-v3.8.ajd architecture; deleted along with the redirect. **Scope:** ~70 LOC net across 3 files (backend reference generation + frontend state + success screen card insertion + redirect removal). **Pre-commit gates:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean (`/onboarding` 17.5 → 17.8 kB). **Banked v3.8.ajr (next):** Step 3 required-documents upload silent-fail since v3.8.ajd. Pre-ajd registration auto-issued a JWT so the post-submit `POST /api/carrier/documents` with `Bearer ${data.token}` worked. Post-ajd the doc upload silently 401s. v3.8.ajr will accept multipart on the register endpoint OR add a verification-link-authenticated upload path. ~120 LOC. **Letter:** ajp latest HEAD; ajq sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajp | 2026-05-24 | **Chameleon match inline alert in SecuritySignalsCard.** Surfaces OPEN/REVIEWED chameleon matches (carrier fingerprint overlap with other carriers by phone / email / address / EIN / IP / DOT) at the top of the security-signals surface, ABOVE the geo-mismatch alert — identity-cluster fraud is higher-priority than country-jump. Pre-ajp the chameleon matches existed in `chameleonDetectionService` but weren't surfaced alongside the carrier's other security context; AE had to navigate to a separate UI. **Backend:** `GET /carriers/:id/security-signals` endpoint extended with a `chameleonMatches` query against the existing `ChameleonMatch` model (scoped by `carrierId`, filtered to `status IN ("OPEN", "REVIEWED")`, ordered newest-first, capped at 10). Each match returns `matchType` (PHONE/EMAIL/ADDRESS/EIN/IP/DOT/MULTI) + `riskScore` (0-100) + `status` + `createdAt` + matched carrier identity (id/companyName/MC#/DOT#/onboardingStatus). **Frontend:** new red-bordered alert card at top of SecuritySignalsCard. Header shows count + brief explanation pointing AE to the chameleon UI for full triage. Each match renders as a sub-row with matchType pill + risk score + UNREVIEWED label (OPEN only) + matched-carrier identity inline. No new schema, no migration — uses existing chameleon infrastructure. **Scope:** ~80 LOC across backend endpoint extension + frontend interface + render block. **Pre-commit gates:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **Letter:** ajo → ajp. |

| v3.8.ajo | 2026-05-24 | **Extended security timeline (Document/OtpCode events) + AE geo-mismatch override action (bundled — both extend the SecuritySignalsCard surface).** **Schema:** 3 new nullable columns on `carrier_profiles` for the override audit trail: `geoMismatchOverriddenAt` + `geoMismatchOverriddenById` + `geoMismatchOverrideNote`. **Backend `GET /carriers/:id/security-signals` extension:** (a) timeline now merges three sources — existing SystemLog events from emailVerification + carrierAuth-unusual-activity, recent Document uploads (uploadSource=CARRIER_PORTAL, last 10), recent failed OTP attempts (failedAttempts > 0, last 10) — normalized to a unified shape with `type: "SYSTEM_LOG" | "DOCUMENT_UPLOAD" | "OTP_FAILURE"`, merged + sorted newest-first + capped at 50. (b) `geoMismatch` flag now AND'd with `!overriddenAt` so override suppresses the alert; `rawMismatch` + `overriddenAt` + `overrideNote` also returned for UI to render the "Overridden by AE" pill state. **New endpoint `POST /carriers/:id/override-mismatch`** (ADMIN/CEO + audit-logged + min-5-char justification required). **SecuritySignalsCard frontend extension:** override action expanded inline when mismatch alert pill renders — Override link → small textarea + Confirm button → POST to override endpoint → TanStack invalidation refetches and the alert disappears. When the override is already in place, a gray "Country mismatch overridden by AE" pill with the note (italicized in quotes) + timestamp surfaces in place of the warning. Timeline events get type-tag icons (FileText for DOCUMENT_UPLOAD, KeyRound amber for OTP_FAILURE) inline with severity badge so AE can scan event categories at a glance. **Scope:** ~280 LOC across schema + 1 migration + carriers route extensions + SecuritySignalsCard rewrite + 1-line call-site update. **Pre-commit gates:** prisma generate + backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **Letter:** ajn → ajo. |

| v3.8.ajn | 2026-05-24 | **Lift Rejection — AE action to restore mistakenly-rejected carriers.** Used when AE picks wrong reason or new info means rejection is no longer warranted. **Service `rejectionService.liftCarrierRejection()`:** validates currently REJECTED, clears all 5 rejection fields (rejectionReason/rejectedAt/rejectedById/rejectionNote/reapplyEligibleAt) + the ajm `reapplyReminderSentAt` dedup, flips status REJECTED → REVIEWING, sends carrier email notifying them their application is back under review with optional AE note. **New endpoint `POST /api/carriers/:id/lift-rejection`** (ADMIN/CEO, Zod-validated optional 2000-char note, audit-logged via `auditLog("UPDATE", "Carrier")`). **AE UI:** new blue Lift Rejection button on `/dashboard/carriers` action bar, only shown when `onboardingStatus === "REJECTED"`. `confirm()` dialog inline (no separate modal — single decision, no field capture needed for the v1 ship; note field could be added later if AE finds it useful). On success invalidates carriers query so the status pill flips back to REVIEWING in the list. **Scope:** ~60 LOC across rejectionService + carriers route + carriers page button. **Pre-commit gates:** backend tsc + frontend tsc + next build all clean. **Letter:** ajm → ajn sequence-continuous. |

| v3.8.ajm | 2026-05-24 | **Reapply-eligibility reminder cron.** Daily 8am Eastern (timezone-pinned per Item 185 convention) cron finds REJECTED carriers whose `reapplyEligibleAt <= now` AND `reapplyReminderSentAt IS NULL` AND `deletedAt IS NULL`, emails them a "you can reapply now" notification with `/onboarding` CTA, marks `reapplyReminderSentAt` to dedup. Permanent rejections (null `reapplyEligibleAt` from FRAUD_DETECTED/IDENTITY_FRAUD) skipped by the `lte: now, not: null` filter. Per-carrier email send failures logged but don't block the batch — partial completions are acceptable, next-day cron retries any unmarked rows. 100-carrier safety cap per tick. **Schema:** 1 nullable column `carrier_profiles.reapplyReminderSentAt`. Manual migration per §2.2. **Banking note:** v3.8.ajn will clear this field on Lift Rejection so a future re-rejection's reminder still fires on its next eligible cycle. **Scope:** ~65 LOC across schema + 1 migration + cron/index.ts cron block. **Pre-commit gates:** prisma generate + backend tsc clean. **Letter:** ajl latest HEAD; ajm sequence-continuous. |

Response includes both `request` (resolved) + `attachments` (id/fileName/fileUrl) arrays. **Service layer (`infoRequestService.ts`):** `resolveInfoRequest` signature extended with optional `attachmentCount` arg (defaults to 0); passed through to `sendInfoRequestResolvedEmail`.
